math-holder">\nabla\cdot A=\frac{1}{J}\partial_k(JA^k)</span></span></span> ，上指标表示原矢量在各个单位矢量上的分量</p><p data-pid="f4Poy7Zr"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla \frac{1}{r}=-\frac{1}{r^2}e_r"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla \frac{1}{r}=-\frac{1}{r^2}e_r</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="TlTKp1mF"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla^2\frac{1}{r}=\nabla \cdot \nabla\frac{1}{r}=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla^2\frac{1}{r}=\nabla \cdot \nabla\frac{1}{r}=0</span></span></span> , <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="(r\ne0)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">(r\ne0)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="sI0z8r_I"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla^2\frac{1}{r}=-4\pi\delta^3(x)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla^2\frac{1}{r}=-4\pi\delta^3(x)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="0NBI6ogh">重要： <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla_x^2\frac{1}{4\pi|x-x'|}=-\delta^3(x-x')"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla_x^2\frac{1}{4\pi|x-x'|}=-\delta^3(x-x')</span></span></span> </p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><p data-pid="-UjgWzQa"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla_x\phi(x-x')=(e_x\partial_x+e_y\partial_y+e_z\partial_z)\phi(x-x')"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla_x\phi(x-x')=(e_x\partial_x+e_y\partial_y+e_z\partial_z)\phi(x-x')</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="Eu634wWW"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla_x\phi(x-x')=(e_x\partial_{x'}+e_y\partial_{y'}+e_z\partial_{z'})\phi(x-x')"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla_x\phi(x-x')=(e_x\partial_{x'}+e_y\partial_{y'}+e_z\partial_{z'})\phi(x-x')</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="4xnvPGGX"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla_R\phi(R)=(e_x\partial_{R_x}+e_y\partial_{R_y}+e_z\partial_{R_z})\phi(R)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla_R\phi(R)=(e_x\partial_{R_x}+e_y\partial_{R_y}+e_z\partial_{R_z})\phi(R)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="7uu6sXOe">故 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla_x=-\nabla_{x'}=\nabla_R"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla_x=-\nabla_{x'}=\nabla_R</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla_x^2=\nabla_{x'}^2=\nabla_R^2"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla_x^2=\nabla_{x'}^2=\nabla_R^2</span></span></span> </p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>2.<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E4%BA%A5%E5%A7%86%E9%9C%8D%E5%85%B9%E5%AE%9A%E7%90%86&amp;zhida_source=entity" F(x)=\rho(x)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla\cdot F(x)=\rho(x)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="yRcxa4bd"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla\times F(x)=j(x)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla\times F(x)=j(x)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="01Ao8u-3"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="F(x)=-\nabla\phi(x)+\nabla\times A(x)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">F(x)=-\nabla\phi(x)+\nabla\times A(x)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="R-Mo-Ugr"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\phi(x)=\frac{1}{4\pi}\int d^3x'\rho(x')\frac{1}{|x-x'|}\\ \textbf{A}(x)=\frac{1}{4\pi}\int d^3x'j(x')\frac{1}{|x-x'|}\\"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\phi(x)=\frac{1}{4\pi}\int d^3x'\rho(x')\frac{1}{|x-x'|}\\ \textbf{A}(x)=\frac{1}{4\pi}\int d^3x'j(x')\frac{1}{|x-x'|}\\</span></span></span></p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>3.<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E4%BA%8C%E9%98%B6%E5%BC%A0%E9%87%8F&amp;zhida_source=entity" 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>计算</h3><p data-pid="54PXShv6"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\mathbf{P}=e_ie_jP_{ij}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\mathbf{P}=e_ie_jP_{ij}</span></span></span> ,</p><p data-pid="BsN-COIO"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="A\cdot\textbf{P}=A_ke_k\cdot e_ie_jP_{ij}=A_ie_jP_{ij}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">A\cdot\textbf{P}=A_ke_k\cdot e_ie_jP_{ij}=A_ie_jP_{ij}</span></span></span> ,     <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\textbf{P}\cdot A=e_ie_jP_{ij}=e_ie_jP_{ij}\cdot A_ke_k=e_iP_{ij}A_k"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\textbf{P}\cdot A=e_ie_jP_{ij}=e_ie_jP_{ij}\cdot A_ke_k=e_iP_{ij}A_k</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="zxbxGixy">仍然有<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E6%95%A3%E5%BA%A6%E5%AE%9A%E7%90%86&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">散度定理<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span> <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla\cdot P=e_i\partial_i \cdot e_ie_jP_{ij}=\partial_ie_jP_{ij}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla\cdot P=e_i\partial_i \cdot e_ie_jP_{ij}=\partial_ie_jP_{ij}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="GnA4colQ"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla\cdot (\textbf{AB})=(\nabla\cdot A)B+(A\cdot \nabla )B"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla\cdot (\textbf{AB})=(\nabla\cdot A)B+(A\cdot \nabla )B</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="uzJXVJcF"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="(A\cdot \nabla) B=(e_iA_i\cdot e_j\partial_j)e_k B_k=A_i\partial_i e_kB_k=A_ie_i\cdot e_i\partial_ie_kB_k=A\cdot(\nabla B)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">(A\cdot \nabla) B=(e_iA_i\cdot e_j\partial_j)e_k B_k=A_i\partial_i e_kB_k=A_ie_i\cdot e_i\partial_ie_kB_k=A\cdot(\nabla B)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="BMH1gKRH"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="I=\nabla x=e_i\partial_i e_j x_j=e_ie_i\partial x_j=e_ie_i"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">I=\nabla x=e_i\partial_i e_j x_j=e_ie_i\partial x_j=e_ie_i</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="SR90DltK"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="A\cdot \textbf{I}=\textbf{I}\cdot A =A"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">A\cdot \textbf{I}=\textbf{I}\cdot A =A</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="8aNPSpnu"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="A\times(B\times C)=(C\cdot A)B-(B\cdot A)C=A(CB-BC)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">A\times(B\times C)=(C\cdot A)B-(B\cdot A)C=A(CB-BC)</span></span></span> </p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>4.积分变形</h3><p data-pid="tZdvpcuW"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\int dS\cdot \nabla\times F=\int dl\cdot F"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\int dS\cdot \nabla\times F=\int dl\cdot F</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="fduWzfnQ"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\int d^3x( \nabla \cdot F)=\int dS\cdot F"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\int d^3x( \nabla \cdot F)=\int dS\cdot F</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="wm05St6K"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\int d^3x (\nabla\times F)=\int dS \times F"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\int d^3x (\nabla\times F)=\int dS \times F</span></span></span> </p><hr><h2><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="Chapter \ \ 2"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">Chapter \ \ 2</span></span></span> </h2><h3>1.真空电场</h3><p data-pid="U2cV6GA0"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="E=\frac{1}{4\pi\epsilon_0}\int d^3x'\rho(x')\frac{x-x'}{|x-x'|^3}\\"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">E=\frac{1}{4\pi\epsilon_0}\int d^3x'\rho(x')\frac{x-x'}{|x-x'|^3}\\</span></span></span><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\phi(x)=\frac{1}{4\pi\epsilon_0}\int d^3x'\rho(x')\frac{1}{|x-x'|}\\"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\phi(x)=\frac{1}{4\pi\epsilon_0}\int d^3x'\rho(x')\frac{1}{|x-x'|}\\</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="ehW76Fag"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla\times E=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla\times E=0</span></span></span> ,得到 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\oint E\cdot dl=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\oint E\cdot dl=0</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="RY7UiiSk"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla\cdot E=\frac{\rho(x)}{\epsilon_0}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla\cdot E=\frac{\rho(x)}{\epsilon_0}</span></span></span> , 得到 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\oint E\cdot dS=\frac{Q}{\epsilon_0}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\oint E\cdot dS=\frac{Q}{\epsilon_0}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="zdhLjMzu"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\partial_t \rho+\nabla\cdot j=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\partial_t \rho+\nabla\cdot j=0</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="fA0_98UD"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\rho(x,t)=Q\delta^3[x-x_e(t)]"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\rho(x,t)=Q\delta^3[x-x_e(t)]</span></span></span> ，左右对时间求<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E5%81%8F%E5%AF%BC%E6%95%B0&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">偏导数<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span></p><p data-pid="9sKiK0Ni">得 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="j=Q\frac{dx_e}{dt}\delta^3[x-x_e(t)]"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">j=Q\frac{dx_e}{dt}\delta^3[x-x_e(t)]</span></span></span> </p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>2.真空磁场</h3><p data-pid="8Cyt9Gbi"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="dF=Idl\times(\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi})(I'dl'\times\frac{x-x'}{|x-x'|^3})"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">dF=Idl\times(\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi})(I'dl'\times\frac{x-x'}{|x-x'|^3})</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="OXD_FVVq"><span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E6%AF%95%E5%A5%A5%E8%90%A8%E6%B3%95%E5%B0%94%E5%AE%9A%E5%BE%8B&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">毕奥萨法尔定律<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>： <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="dB=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}Idl'\times\frac{x-x'}{|x-x'|^3}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">dB=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}Idl'\times\frac{x-x'}{|x-x'|^3}</span></span></span> ，在导线上时 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\int jd^3x=\int Idl'"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\int jd^3x=\int Idl'</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="KMD7E2_P">电流对单位体积磁场作用力： <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="f_A=j\times B"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">f_A=j\times B</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="PJxuFwDS">故 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="f=\rho E+j\times B"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">f=\rho E+j\times B</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="3MT85JyB"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="B=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\int Idl'\times\frac{x-x'}{|x-x'|^3}=\nabla\times(\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\int \frac{Idl'}{|x-x'|})"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">B=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\int Idl'\times\frac{x-x'}{|x-x'|^3}=\nabla\times(\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\int \frac{Idl'}{|x-x'|})</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="bF1IgTQB">把后面一项令为 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="A"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">A</span></span></span>  ，即为<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E7%A3%81%E7%9F%A2%E5%8A%BF&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">磁矢势<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span></p><p data-pid="fUyUu5cM">所以 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla\cdot B=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla\cdot B=0</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="26rsoViL"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla\times B=\mu_0j"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla\times B=\mu_0j</span></span></span> (需要一定量的计算）即 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\int_\Sigma dS\cdot\nabla \times B=\mu_0\int_\Sigma dS\cdot j"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\int_\Sigma dS\cdot\nabla \times B=\mu_0\int_\Sigma dS\cdot j</span></span></span></p><p data-pid="bsVf8TjZ">即 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\oint_L dl\cdot B=\mu_0I"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\oint_L dl\cdot B=\mu_0I</span></span></span> </p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>3.Faraday<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E7%94%B5%E7%A3%81%E6%84%9F%E5%BA%94%E5%AE%9A%E5%BE%8B&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">电磁感应定律<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span></h3><p data-pid="kLk5GRWK"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\epsilon=RI=-\frac{\Delta\Phi}{\Delta t}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\epsilon=RI=-\frac{\Delta\Phi}{\Delta t}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="y3UyHj6K">1.感生电动势 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\epsilon=\oint_Ldl\cdot E=\int_{\Sigma}dS\cdot\nabla\times E"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\epsilon=\oint_Ldl\cdot E=\int_{\Sigma}dS\cdot\nabla\times E</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="vCE9guHc">又有固定面积时 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\epsilon=-\frac{d}{dt}\int_{\Sigma}dS\cdot B=-\int_\Sigma dS\cdot \partial_tB"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\epsilon=-\frac{d}{dt}\int_{\Sigma}dS\cdot B=-\int_\Sigma dS\cdot \partial_tB</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="b6qMhlWV">即 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla\times E=-\partial_t B"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla\times E=-\partial_t B</span></span></span> （法拉第方程）</p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><p data-pid="mnWyer90">2.动生电动势</p><p data-pid="n1BBD17M"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\frac{\Delta\Phi}{\Delta t}=\int_\Sigma dS\cdot\partial_tB+\oint_Ldl\cdot (B\times u)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\frac{\Delta\Phi}{\Delta t}=\int_\Sigma dS\cdot\partial_tB+\oint_Ldl\cdot (B\times u)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="sl2-DJej"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\epsilon=-\frac{\Delta \Phi}{\Delta t}=\oint_Ldl\cdot E'"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\epsilon=-\frac{\Delta \Phi}{\Delta t}=\oint_Ldl\cdot E'</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="wt7d9Tlk"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\oint_Ldl\cdot E'=-\int_\Sigma dS\cdot\partial_tB-\oint_Ldl\cdot (B\times u\Delta t)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\oint_Ldl\cdot E'=-\int_\Sigma dS\cdot\partial_tB-\oint_Ldl\cdot (B\times u\Delta t)</span></span></span> </p><figure data-size="normal"><div class="RichText-ConditionalImagePortal"><img src="https://pic4.zhimg.com/v2-2c5bbaf6067319d69ec51095169621c3_1440w.jpg" data-caption="" data-size="normal" data-rawwidth="1106" data-rawheight="779" data-original-token="v2-6678bec154975b68c4f52cd0ad26e56d" class="origin_image zh-lightbox-thumb" width="1106" data-original="https://pic4.zhimg.com/v2-2c5bbaf6067319d69ec51095169621c3_r.jpg"></div></figure><p data-pid="NktDn8E_"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla\times(E'-u\times B)=-\partial_t B"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla\times(E'-u\times B)=-\partial_t B</span></span></span><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\oint_Ldl\cdot E'=-\int_\Sigma dS\cdot\nabla\times E-\oint_Ldl\cdot (B\times u\Delta t)\\ =-\oint_Ldl\cdot E-\oint_Ldl\cdot (B\times u\Delta t)\\"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\oint_Ldl\cdot E'=-\int_\Sigma dS\cdot\nabla\times E-\oint_Ldl\cdot (B\times u\Delta t)\\ =-\oint_Ldl\cdot E-\oint_Ldl\cdot (B\times u\Delta t)\\</span></span></span></p><p data-pid="9JS7fYwh"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="E'=E+u\times B"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">E'=E+u\times B</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="Nrja9F2R"><b>（可以想一想，这个证明里单位统一自然吗（<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E7%A3%81%E9%80%9A&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">磁通<svg width="10px" height="10px" 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>导出的电动势和<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=2&amp;q=%E7%94%B5%E5%9C%BA&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">电场<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>导出的电动势单位）其实不自然））</b></p><p data-pid="PbpZYuJt">所以得到<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E9%BA%A6%E5%85%8B%E6%96%AF%E9%9F%A6%E6%96%B9%E7%A8%8B%E7%BB%84&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">麦克斯韦方程组<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span> </p><p data-pid="DfXg-KLT"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla\cdot E=\frac{\rho}{\epsilon_0}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla\cdot E=\frac{\rho}{\epsilon_0}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="bhjOGhe2"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla\times E=-\partial_tB"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla\times E=-\partial_tB</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="_Nrpa2SK"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla\cdot B=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla\cdot B=0</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="H5g8eUpF"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla\times B=\mu_0j"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla\times B=\mu_0j</span></span></span> （不太对？这样求散度 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla\cdot j=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla\cdot j=0</span></span></span> 而 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\partial_t \rho+\nabla\cdot j=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\partial_t \rho+\nabla\cdot j=0</span></span></span> ）</p><p data-pid="zEnU_sX9">所以修改为 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla\times B=\mu_0j+\mu_0\epsilon_0\partial_t E"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla\times B=\mu_0j+\mu_0\epsilon_0\partial_t E</span></span></span> </p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>4.<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=Lorentz%E5%8A%9B&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">Lorentz力<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span></h3><p data-pid="MicCvJa7">公式: <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\frac{dp}{dt}=q(E+u\times B)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\frac{dp}{dt}=q(E+u\times B)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="zaug3Ovi">都除以体积 有 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="f=\rho E+\rho u\times B=\rho E+j\times B"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">f=\rho E+\rho u\times B=\rho E+j\times B</span></span></span> </p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>5.静止真空方程总结</h3><p data-pid="pU7WmvQw"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla\cdot E=\frac{\rho}{\epsilon_0}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla\cdot E=\frac{\rho}{\epsilon_0}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="MQft9CCN"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla\times E=-\partial_tB"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla\times E=-\partial_tB</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="KKvYyYDi"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla\cdot B=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla\cdot B=0</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="6PGfpMbj"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla\times B=\mu_0j+\mu_0\epsilon_0\partial_t E"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla\times B=\mu_0j+\mu_0\epsilon_0\partial_t E</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="_4zQLSAB"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\oint_\Sigma dS\cdot E=\frac{Q}{\epsilon_0}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\oint_\Sigma dS\cdot E=\frac{Q}{\epsilon_0}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="YJkNaglU"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\oint_{\partial \Sigma} dl\cdot E=-\partial_t\oint_\Sigma dS\cdot B"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\oint_{\partial \Sigma} dl\cdot E=-\partial_t\oint_\Sigma dS\cdot B</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="J955J4ht"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\oint_\Sigma dS\cdot B=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\oint_\Sigma dS\cdot B=0</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="bFMxjunz"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\oint_{\partial \Sigma} dl\cdot B=\mu_0I+\mu_0\epsilon_0\partial_t\oint_\Sigma dS\cdot E"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\oint_{\partial \Sigma} dl\cdot B=\mu_0I+\mu_0\epsilon_0\partial_t\oint_\Sigma dS\cdot E</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="HRgzajPE"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\partial_t\rho+\nabla\cdot j=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\partial_t\rho+\nabla\cdot j=0</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="ISEmwAGI"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="f=\rho E+j\times B"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">f=\rho E+j\times B</span></span></span> </p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>6.介质场性质</h3><p data-pid="BRHU0EyX">实验中发现E在有电介质插入时变小，故引入了 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\rho_{f}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\rho_{f}</span></span></span> ，这样第一个式子变成</p><p data-pid="Q1X2ewwg"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla\cdot E=\frac{1}{\epsilon_0}(\rho_f+\rho_p)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla\cdot E=\frac{1}{\epsilon_0}(\rho_f+\rho_p)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="jNVPwYKD">因为 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\rho"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\rho</span></span></span> 有对应的散度表示，引入<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E6%9E%81%E5%8C%96%E5%BC%BA%E5%BA%A6%E7%9F%A2%E9%87%8F&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">极化强度矢量<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span> <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="P"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">P</span></span></span> 让 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\rho_p"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\rho_p</span></span></span> 也有散度表示，即 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="-\nabla\cdot P=\rho_p"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">-\nabla\cdot P=\rho_p</span></span></span> , <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="-"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">-</span></span></span> 是人为加的</p><p data-pid="LHZ_F-uT">那么得到 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla \cdot (\epsilon_0E+P)=\rho_f"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla \cdot (\epsilon_0E+P)=\rho_f</span></span></span> ，让括号内的为新矢量（<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E7%94%B5%E4%BD%8D%E7%A7%BB%E7%9F%A2%E9%87%8F&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">电位移矢量<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>） <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="D=\epsilon_0E+P"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">D=\epsilon_0E+P</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="vMoqV-rz">大部分各向同性介质里 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="P \propto E"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">P \propto E</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="P=\epsilon_0\chi_EE，D=\epsilon_0\epsilon_rE=(1+\chi_E)\epsilon_0E"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">P=\epsilon_0\chi_EE，D=\epsilon_0\epsilon_rE=(1+\chi_E)\epsilon_0E</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="wvhxDGnX"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla\cdot D=\rho_f"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla\cdot D=\rho_f</span></span></span> , 代入散度定理， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\oint_\Sigma dS\cdot D=Q_f"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\oint_\Sigma dS\cdot D=Q_f</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="Y4sxmVMy">对于 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="P"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">P</span></span></span> 也有， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\int d^3x\rho_p=-\oint dS\cdot P"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\int d^3x\rho_p=-\oint dS\cdot P</span></span></span> , 此时让取的区域厚度 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\to 0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\to 0</span></span></span> ，得到 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\rho_p S\Delta z=-Sn\cdot(P_2-P_1)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\rho_p S\Delta z=-Sn\cdot(P_2-P_1)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="AK9bfwlJ">因为 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\rho_p\Delta z=\sigma_p"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\rho_p\Delta z=\sigma_p</span></span></span> , 得到 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\sigma_p=-n\cdot (P_2-P_1)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\sigma_p=-n\cdot (P_2-P_1)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="D-iC52GF"><span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E7%94%B5%E5%81%B6%E6%9E%81%E7%9F%A9&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">电偶极矩<svg <span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" target="_blank">电极化强度<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span> <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="P=\lim_{\Delta V=0}\frac{\Sigma_{V内} p}{\Delta V}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">P=\lim_{\Delta V=0}\frac{\Sigma_{V内} p}{\Delta V}</span></span></span> </p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><p data-pid="jAuvrY0w">对于磁电流：一样设出 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="j_M"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">j_M</span></span></span> (<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E7%A3%81%E5%8C%96%E7%94%B5%E6%B5%81&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">磁化电流<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>)， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla\times B=\mu_0(j_f+j_M)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla\times B=\mu_0(j_f+j_M)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="YiMzHlEP">也给 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="j_M"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">j_M</span></span></span> 一个<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E6%97%8B%E5%BA%A6&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">旋度<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>表示 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="j_M=\nabla\times M"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">j_M=\nabla\times M</span></span></span> （<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E7%A3%81%E5%8C%96%E5%BC%BA%E5%BA%A6%E7%9F%A2%E9%87%8F&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">磁化强度矢量<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>）, 这样有 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla\times(\frac{B}{\mu_0}-M)=j_f"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla\times(\frac{B}{\mu_0}-M)=j_f</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="dgh831IT">也给括号里的一个新矢量（<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E7%A3%81%E5%9C%BA%E5%BC%BA%E5%BA%A6%E7%9F%A2%E9%87%8F&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">磁场强度矢量<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>） <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="H=\frac{B}{\mu_0}-M"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">H=\frac{B}{\mu_0}-M</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="4brIWB_5"><span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E7%A3%81%E5%81%B6%E6%9E%81%E7%9F%A9&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">磁偶极矩<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span> <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="m=IS"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">m=IS</span></span></span> ，磁化强度 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="M=\lim_{\Delta V=0}\frac{\Sigma_{V内} m}{\Delta V}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">M=\lim_{\Delta V=0}\frac{\Sigma_{V内} m}{\Delta V}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="Fs_nUrN1">于是 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla\times H=j_f"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla\times H=j_f</span></span></span> , 对 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="j_M"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">j_M</span></span></span> 有 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\int d^3x j_M=\int dS\times M"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\int d^3x j_M=\int dS\times M</span></span></span> (Stokes定理)</p><p data-pid="5giawbST">再取厚度接近0，得到 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="j_M S\Delta z=S\times (M_2-M_1)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">j_M S\Delta z=S\times (M_2-M_1)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="Mkc9WTgO">即 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\alpha_M=n\times(M_2-M_1)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\alpha_M=n\times(M_2-M_1)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="kx1H-GPU">一般情况下 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="M\propto H，M=\chi_MH,B=\mu_0H+\mu_0M=\mu_0(1+\chi_M)H=\mu_0\mu_rH"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">M\propto H，M=\chi_MH,B=\mu_0H+\mu_0M=\mu_0(1+\chi_M)H=\mu_0\mu_rH</span></span></span> </p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><p data-pid="ZFpK7Wkp">另外，<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E6%9E%81%E5%8C%96%E7%94%B5%E8%8D%B7&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">极化电荷<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>的运动会产生极化位移电流，设为 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="j_p=\partial_tP"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">j_p=\partial_tP</span></span></span> （P大小其实是<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E9%9D%A2%E7%94%B5%E8%8D%B7%E5%AF%86%E5%BA%A6&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">面电荷密度<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>）</p><p data-pid="FNUxNPfx"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\partial_t\rho_p+\nabla\cdot j_p=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\partial_t\rho_p+\nabla\cdot j_p=0</span></span></span> , 由 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="D=\epsilon_0E+P "><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">D=\epsilon_0E+P </span></span></span> , <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex=" \partial_tD=\partial_t\epsilon_0E+\partial_tP"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder"> \partial_tD=\partial_t\epsilon_0E+\partial_tP</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="KaySlEDZ">得到 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla\times H=j_f+\partial_t D"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla\times H=j_f+\partial_t D</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="b1EbDFc4">综上：</p><p data-pid="bfmBVinp"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla\times E=-\partial_tB"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla\times E=-\partial_tB</span></span></span></p><p data-pid="WZ2crkn3"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla\cdot B=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla\cdot B=0</span></span></span></p><p data-pid="bwa-0txi"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla\cdot D=\rho_f"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla\cdot D=\rho_f</span></span></span></p><p data-pid="NArXssPk"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla\times H=j_f+\partial_t D"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla\times H=j_f+\partial_t D</span></span></span></p><p data-pid="M3J0iK0O"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\oint_{\partial \Sigma} dl\cdot E=-\partial_t\oint_\Sigma dS\cdot B"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\oint_{\partial \Sigma} dl\cdot E=-\partial_t\oint_\Sigma dS\cdot B</span></span></span></p><p data-pid="QpVgk3-G"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\oint_\Sigma dS\cdot B=0 "><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\oint_\Sigma dS\cdot B=0 </span></span></span></p><p data-pid="FThB70AY"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\oint_\Sigma dS\cdot D=Q_f"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\oint_\Sigma dS\cdot D=Q_f</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="U3wYEk2F"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\oint_{\partial\Sigma}dl\cdot H=I_f+\partial_t\int_\Sigma dS\cdot D"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\oint_{\partial\Sigma}dl\cdot H=I_f+\partial_t\int_\Sigma dS\cdot D</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="6kgqXPjs">还有两个不同形式</p><p data-pid="zgzyu6mq"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\oint_\Sigma dS\cdot E=-\int_V d^3x \partial_t B"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\oint_\Sigma dS\cdot E=-\int_V d^3x \partial_t B</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="mbuSWWnc"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\oint_\Sigma dS\cdot H=\int_V d^3x (j_f+\partial_tD)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\oint_\Sigma dS\cdot H=\int_V d^3x (j_f+\partial_tD)</span></span></span> </p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>7.<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E8%BE%B9%E5%80%BC%E6%9D%A1%E4%BB%B6&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">边值条件<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span></h3><p data-pid="vhRVp-Bc"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\sigma_p=-n\cdot (P_2-P_1)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\sigma_p=-n\cdot (P_2-P_1)</span></span></span></p><p data-pid="sisiZv8c"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\alpha_M=n\times(M_2-M_1)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\alpha_M=n\times(M_2-M_1)</span></span></span></p><p data-pid="zeLYx-it">P为散度定义，M为旋度定义，那么电场相关的散度项非0，磁场相关的旋度项非0</p><p data-pid="7iF14kzE">很容易推出</p><p data-pid="2SbYRL0R"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\sigma_f=n\cdot (D_2-D_1)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\sigma_f=n\cdot (D_2-D_1)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="CHK31u_s"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="0=n\times (E_2-E_1)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">0=n\times (E_2-E_1)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="9FHR48De"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="0=n\cdot (B_2-B_1)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">0=n\cdot (B_2-B_1)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="0fMvLd37"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\alpha_f=n\times (H_2-H_1)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\alpha_f=n\times (H_2-H_1)</span></span></span> </p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>8.<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E7%94%B5%E7%A3%81%E5%9C%BA%E8%83%BD%E9%87%8F&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">电磁场能量<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E6%B4%9B%E4%BC%A6%E5%85%B9%E5%8A%9B&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">洛伦兹力<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span><b>单位体积</b><span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E5%81%9A%E5%8A%9F%E5%8A%9F%E7%8E%87&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">做功功率<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>（狭义的洛伦兹力不做功）</p><p data-pid="klbp0IHg"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="f\cdot u=(\rho_fE+j_f\times B)\cdot u=j_f\cdot E"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">f\cdot u=(\rho_fE+j_f\times B)\cdot u=j_f\cdot E</span></span></span> （看后面那项展开得到是 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="(\rho_fu\times B)\cdot u"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">(\rho_fu\times B)\cdot u</span></span></span> , <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="(u\times B )\bot u"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">(u\times B )\bot u</span></span></span> =0</p><p data-pid="YL0CFdgW">令 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="S"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">S</span></span></span> 为<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E8%83%BD%E6%B5%81%E5%AF%86%E5%BA%A6&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">能流密度<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>（单位时间单位面积做的功）</p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><p data-pid="iNSgHZZN">考虑<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E8%83%BD%E9%87%8F%E5%AE%88%E6%81%92&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">能量守恒<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>： <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="-\oint_\Sigma d\Sigma\cdot S=\partial_t \int_Vd^3x w+\int_Vd^3x j_f\cdot E"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">-\oint_\Sigma d\Sigma\cdot S=\partial_t \int_Vd^3x w+\int_Vd^3x j_f\cdot E</span></span></span> (第一个项的负号是因为面，S的正方向向外)</p><p data-pid="2plJBsxo">即流入能量=内部能量变化+做功（其实和 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="dQ=dU+dW"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">dQ=dU+dW</span></span></span> 对时间<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E6%B1%82%E5%AF%BC&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">求导<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>一样啊）</p><p data-pid="kCMpUcaB">由<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E9%AB%98%E6%96%AF%E5%AE%9A%E7%90%86&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">高斯定理<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span> <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="-\nabla\cdot S=\partial_t w+j_f\cdot E"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">-\nabla\cdot S=\partial_t w+j_f\cdot E</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="h94nnqlR"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="j_f\cdot E=(\nabla\times H-\partial_t D)\cdot E=-\nabla\cdot(E\times H)-(E\cdot \partial_tD+H\cdot\partial_tB)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">j_f\cdot E=(\nabla\times H-\partial_t D)\cdot E=-\nabla\cdot(E\times H)-(E\cdot \partial_tD+H\cdot\partial_tB)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="r3I4OEHO">得到 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="S=E\times H"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">S=E\times H</span></span></span> , <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\partial_t w=E\cdot \partial_tD+H\cdot \partial_t B"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\partial_t w=E\cdot \partial_tD+H\cdot \partial_t B</span></span></span> （其实S还能加上任意<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E6%97%A0%E6%BA%90%E5%9C%BA&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">无源场<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>）</p><p data-pid="lZnpPv5R">真空中 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="S=E\times \frac{B}{\mu_0}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">S=E\times \frac{B}{\mu_0}</span></span></span> , <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="w=\frac{1}{2}\epsilon_0E^2+\frac{1}{2}\frac{B^2}{\mu_0}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">w=\frac{1}{2}\epsilon_0E^2+\frac{1}{2}\frac{B^2}{\mu_0}</span></span></span> </p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>9.电磁场动量与<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E9%BA%A6%E5%85%8B%E6%96%AF%E9%9F%A6%E5%BA%94%E5%8A%9B%E5%BC%A0%E9%87%8F&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">麦克斯韦应力张量<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span></h3><p data-pid="Zttn424A">考虑<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E5%8A%A8%E9%87%8F%E5%AE%88%E6%81%92&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">动量守恒<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>：流入动量=动量增加+给别的物体施力的动量, T为应力张量（单位面积的动量）</p><p data-pid="1vHW9K7y"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="-\oint_{\Sigma}d\Sigma \cdot T=\partial_t\int_Vd^3xg+\int_Vd^3x f"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">-\oint_{\Sigma}d\Sigma \cdot T=\partial_t\int_Vd^3xg+\int_Vd^3x f</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="0An_jcx0">即 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="-\nabla\cdot T=\partial_t g+f"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">-\nabla\cdot T=\partial_t g+f</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="lLcPp6o0"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="f=\rho E+j\times B=\epsilon_0(\nabla\cdot E)E+\frac{1}{\mu_0}(\nabla\times B)\times B-\epsilon_0\partial_tE\times B"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">f=\rho E+j\times B=\epsilon_0(\nabla\cdot E)E+\frac{1}{\mu_0}(\nabla\times B)\times B-\epsilon_0\partial_tE\times B</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="omJRfnth">因为 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="(\nabla\times A)\times A=-\frac{1}{2}\nabla(A\cdot A)+A\cdot \nabla A\\=-\nabla\cdot(\frac{A^2}{2}I)+\nabla\cdot(AA)-(\nabla \cdot A)A=-\nabla\cdot(\frac{A^2}{2}I-AA)-(\nabla\cdot A)A"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">(\nabla\times A)\times A=-\frac{1}{2}\nabla(A\cdot A)+A\cdot \nabla A\\=-\nabla\cdot(\frac{A^2}{2}I)+\nabla\cdot(AA)-(\nabla \cdot A)A=-\nabla\cdot(\frac{A^2}{2}I-AA)-(\nabla\cdot A)A</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="VMssRNYW"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="f=\nabla\cdot [(\frac{B^2}{2\mu_0}+\frac{\epsilon_0E^2}{2})I-(\frac{BB}{\mu_0}+\epsilon_0EE)]-\partial_t(\epsilon_0E\times B)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">f=\nabla\cdot [(\frac{B^2}{2\mu_0}+\frac{\epsilon_0E^2}{2})I-(\frac{BB}{\mu_0}+\epsilon_0EE)]-\partial_t(\epsilon_0E\times B)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="uZxH1Qj5">得到 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="g=\epsilon_0E\times B"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">g=\epsilon_0E\times B</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="T=[(\frac{B^2}{2\mu_0}+\frac{\epsilon_0E^2}{2})I-(\frac{BB}{\mu_0}+\epsilon_0EE)]"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">T=[(\frac{B^2}{2\mu_0}+\frac{\epsilon_0E^2}{2})I-(\frac{BB}{\mu_0}+\epsilon_0EE)]</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="fqdDvUNE">( <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="S=c^2 g"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">S=c^2 g</span></span></span> ) </p><p data-pid="9CZCKnoM">所以 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="F=-\oint dS\cdot T"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">F=-\oint dS\cdot T</span></span></span> （这是真的力）</p><p data-pid="S9eHFqhF"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="f_M=-\nabla\cdot T"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">f_M=-\nabla\cdot T</span></span></span> （单位体积受力）</p><p data-pid="xKDPwB9l">若没有电场 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="f=-\nabla\cdot T=j\times B"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">f=-\nabla\cdot T=j\times B</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="T=\frac{B^2}{2\mu_0}I-\frac{BB}{\mu_0}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">T=\frac{B^2}{2\mu_0}I-\frac{BB}{\mu_0}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="urXc4zeb">流体中 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla P=f"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla P=f</span></span></span> （单位体积受力）</p><hr><h3><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="Chapter \ \ 3"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">Chapter \ \ 3</span></span></span> 静电场</h3><h3>1.基本量导入</h3><p data-pid="fZQHtF3X">由于静电场无旋</p><p data-pid="7kGYC6jh"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="E=-\nabla\phi"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">E=-\nabla\phi</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\oint d\phi =\oint dl\cdot \nabla \phi"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\oint d\phi =\oint dl\cdot \nabla \phi</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="kzd6RDC9"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla\cdot E=\frac{\rho}{\epsilon_0}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla\cdot E=\frac{\rho}{\epsilon_0}</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla\times E=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla\times E=0</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="YjFh-JWk"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\phi(x)=\frac{1}{4\pi\epsilon_0}\int d^3x'\rho(x')\frac{1}{|x-x'|}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\phi(x)=\frac{1}{4\pi\epsilon_0}\int d^3x'\rho(x')\frac{1}{|x-x'|}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="4A1MYeFM"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="w=\frac{1}{2}D\cdot E"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">w=\frac{1}{2}D\cdot E</span></span></span> （仅有这一个公式） <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="W=\int \frac{1}{2}D\cdot Ed^3x=\int \frac{1}{2}\rho\phi d^3x"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">W=\int \frac{1}{2}D\cdot Ed^3x=\int \frac{1}{2}\rho\phi d^3x</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="CErPxxwk">双系统 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="W=\frac{1}{2}\int d^3 x(\rho_1\phi_1+\rho_2\phi_2+2\rho_1\phi_2)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">W=\frac{1}{2}\int d^3 x(\rho_1\phi_1+\rho_2\phi_2+2\rho_1\phi_2)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="oJkYgrD1">大部分问题变为解方程： <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla^2\phi=-\rho_f"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla^2\phi=-\rho_f</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="KUjBjacD">加上边界方程组成的<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E8%BE%B9%E7%95%8C%E6%9D%A1%E4%BB%B6&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">边界条件<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>（按数理方程学的解就行）</p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>2.镜像法</h3><p data-pid="YM5vIklf">平面：等距 负电</p><p data-pid="rjwVkftx">球：系数距离 负系数电</p><p data-pid="DY1UJ3TA">柱：系数距离 负电</p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>3.<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=Green%E6%96%B9%E6%B3%95&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">Green方法<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span></h3><p data-pid="lU5tSUPg">等我学完数理方程那边Green再看</p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>4.<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E7%94%B5%E5%A4%9A%E6%9E%81%E7%9F%A9&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">电多极矩<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>（important!)</h3><p data-pid="EkT4dcpg"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\phi(x)=\frac{1}{4\pi \epsilon_0}\int d^3 x'\rho(x')\frac{1}{|x-x'|}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\phi(x)=\frac{1}{4\pi \epsilon_0}\int d^3 x'\rho(x')\frac{1}{|x-x'|}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="G6zMVBhn">泰勒展开得到： <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\phi(x)=\int d^3 x'\rho(x')(1-x'\cdot \nabla +\frac{1}{2}x'x':\nabla\nabla)\frac{1}{|x|}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\phi(x)=\int d^3 x'\rho(x')(1-x'\cdot \nabla +\frac{1}{2}x'x':\nabla\nabla)\frac{1}{|x|}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="IMTTq5if"><b>（这里-号是因为位移是 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="-x'"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">-x'</span></span></span> )所以偶数项为负，奇数为正</b></p><p data-pid="WagtNIsh"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="x'x':\nabla\nabla=x_i'x_j'\partial_{ij}^2"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">x'x':\nabla\nabla=x_i'x_j'\partial_{ij}^2</span></span></span> ,这里 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\partial_{ij}^2"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\partial_{ij}^2</span></span></span> 应该是指 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\partial_i\partial_j"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\partial_i\partial_j</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="XGJDreH1"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="Q=\int d^3 x'\rho(x')"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">Q=\int d^3 x'\rho(x')</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="OXb2lUoL"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="p=\int d^3 x'\rho(x')x'"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">p=\int d^3 x'\rho(x')x'</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="Y7x48pTn"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="D=\int d^3x' \rho(x')3x'x'"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">D=\int d^3x' \rho(x')3x'x'</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="o9zwkapH"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\phi(x)=\frac{1}{4\pi \epsilon_0}(Q-p\cdot \nabla+\frac{1}{6}D:\nabla \nabla)\frac{1}{|x|}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\phi(x)=\frac{1}{4\pi \epsilon_0}(Q-p\cdot \nabla+\frac{1}{6}D:\nabla \nabla)\frac{1}{|x|}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="C_VqgG2o"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="I:\nabla\nabla=x_ie_ix_je_je_ie_j\partial_i\partial_j, i,j可以理解为行和列"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">I:\nabla\nabla=x_ie_ix_je_je_ie_j\partial_i\partial_j, i,j可以理解为行和列</span></span></span> 所以 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="I:\nabla\nabla=x_ix_j\partial_i\partial_j,但x_ix_j仅在i=j时为1\\ I:\nabla\nabla=\delta_{ij}\partial_i\partial_j=\partial_i^2=\nabla^2"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">I:\nabla\nabla=x_ix_j\partial_i\partial_j,但x_ix_j仅在i=j时为1\\ I:\nabla\nabla=\delta_{ij}\partial_i\partial_j=\partial_i^2=\nabla^2</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="sK5cVRyv">又因为 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla^2\frac{1}{|x|}=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla^2\frac{1}{|x|}=0</span></span></span> ，所以可以修改 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="D=\int \rho(x')(3x'x'-|x'|^2I)dx'"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">D=\int \rho(x')(3x'x'-|x'|^2I)dx'</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="2SLpUhc2">这样球对称原点体系下 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="D=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">D=0</span></span></span> ，任何时候 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="D_{ii}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">D_{ii}</span></span></span> 即<b>对角线上相加和</b>为0</p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>5.小体积电荷在外场中能量</h3><p data-pid="tRc_ipzG"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="W=\int d^3x'\rho(x')\phi_{ex}(x')=\int d^3x\rho(x')(1+x'\cdot \nabla +\frac{1}{2}x'x':\nabla\nabla)\phi_{ex}(0)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">W=\int d^3x'\rho(x')\phi_{ex}(x')=\int d^3x\rho(x')(1+x'\cdot \nabla +\frac{1}{2}x'x':\nabla\nabla)\phi_{ex}(0)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="7xq3Ycp6">（这里位移式 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">0</span></span></span> 到 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="x'"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">x'</span></span></span> 为 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="+x'"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">+x'</span></span></span> ,没有符号问题）</p><p data-pid="QZrY_aVw"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="W=Q\phi_{ex}(0)+p\cdot\nabla\phi_{ex}(0)+\frac{1}{6}D:\nabla\nabla\phi_{ex}(0)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">W=Q\phi_{ex}(0)+p\cdot\nabla\phi_{ex}(0)+\frac{1}{6}D:\nabla\nabla\phi_{ex}(0)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="FXcCwK6S">受力F: <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="F=-\nabla W_p=p\cdot \nabla E_{ex}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">F=-\nabla W_p=p\cdot \nabla E_{ex}</span></span></span> （对于<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E7%94%B5%E5%81%B6%E6%9E%81%E5%AD%90&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">电偶极子<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>）</p><p data-pid="58oAwTlw">力矩M: <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="M=-\partial_\theta W_p="><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">M=-\partial_\theta W_p=</span></span></span> <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="-\partial_\theta (-pE_{ex}\cos\theta)=-pE_{ex}\sin\theta"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">-\partial_\theta (-pE_{ex}\cos\theta)=-pE_{ex}\sin\theta</span></span></span> （电偶极子由-指向+）</p><p data-pid="T3UszFnJ">考虑方向后自然得到 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\textbf{M}=\bf{p}\times E_{ex}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\textbf{M}=\bf{p}\times E_{ex}</span></span></span> </p><hr><h2><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="Chapter \ \ 4"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">Chapter \ \ 4</span></span></span> 稳恒磁场</h2><h3>1.基本方程</h3><p data-pid="R3FeU1a1"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla\cdot B=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla\cdot B=0</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="NJvG6I08"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla\times H=j_f"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla\times H=j_f</span></span></span> ,不考虑电场项</p><p data-pid="8S16zP6D"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="B=\mu_0H+\mu_0M"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">B=\mu_0H+\mu_0M</span></span></span> 在非铁磁情况下 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="=\mu H"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">=\mu H</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="mKZVgexR">有边值关系 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="n\cdot(B_2-B_1)=0，n\times (H_2-H_1)=\alpha_f"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">n\cdot(B_2-B_1)=0，n\times (H_2-H_1)=\alpha_f</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="gNsZw79p"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla\times(\nabla\times\textbf{A})=\nabla(\nabla\cdot\textbf{A})-\nabla^2\textbf{A}=-\nabla^2A=\mu_0j"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla\times(\nabla\times\textbf{A})=\nabla(\nabla\cdot\textbf{A})-\nabla^2\textbf{A}=-\nabla^2A=\mu_0j</span></span></span> 完全类比静电学的电势</p><p data-pid="i6qCi0Rs">带回得 (记一下）<span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="A(x)=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\int d^3x' j(x')\frac{1}{|x-x'|}\\"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">A(x)=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\int d^3x' j(x')\frac{1}{|x-x'|}\\</span></span></span>又因为 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla\cdot j=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla\cdot j=0</span></span></span> (稳恒），设置 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla\cdot A=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla\cdot A=0</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="vfHwoekV">对于各向同性介质， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla\times(\nabla\times A)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla\times(\nabla\times A)</span></span></span>= <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\mu j_f"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\mu j_f</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="L2HOXqip">对于介质中的磁场，则有 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla^2A=-\mu j_f"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla^2A=-\mu j_f</span></span></span> ，A在边界散度旋度均为0</p><p data-pid="P1LCywaX"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="A_1=A_2"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">A_1=A_2</span></span></span> </p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>2. 电磁场的能量</h3><p data-pid="S6vufxqX"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="w=\frac{1}{2}B\cdot H"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">w=\frac{1}{2}B\cdot H</span></span></span> （不能写成 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\frac{1}{2}j\cdot A"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\frac{1}{2}j\cdot A</span></span></span> )</p><p data-pid="RCE0U78Z">和电场类似， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="W"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">W</span></span></span> 有两种表示方法： <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="W=\int d^3 x\frac{1}{2}B\cdot H"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">W=\int d^3 x\frac{1}{2}B\cdot H</span></span></span> , <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\int d^3 x \frac{1}{2}j\cdot A"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\int d^3 x \frac{1}{2}j\cdot A</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="r0AkvbQN">相互电流能： <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\int d^3 x j_1\cdot A_2"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\int d^3 x j_1\cdot A_2</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="58TZa_hF">于是电流在外场中的能量： <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="W=\int d^3 xj\cdot A_{ex}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">W=\int d^3 xj\cdot A_{ex}</span></span></span> （想象外场是由另外一个线圈发出的）</p><p data-pid="bjfeR1Gn">对于载流线圈： <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="W=I\oint dl\cdot A_{ex}=I\oint dS\cdot (\nabla\times A_{ex})=I\oint dS\cdot B"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">W=I\oint dl\cdot A_{ex}=I\oint dS\cdot (\nabla\times A_{ex})=I\oint dS\cdot B</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="xwrMknEg">即 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="W=I\Phi"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">W=I\Phi</span></span></span> ，当B一定时，可改写为 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="W=m\cdot B"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">W=m\cdot B</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="HJobAkQD">能量也可以为 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\frac{1}{2}LI^2，\frac{1}{2}CU^2"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\frac{1}{2}LI^2，\frac{1}{2}CU^2</span></span></span> 等等，与W联立可解出体系 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="L,C"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">L,C</span></span></span> 等等（ <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="L=\frac{d\Phi}{dt}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">L=\frac{d\Phi}{dt}</span></span></span> ）</p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>3.<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E7%A3%81%E6%A0%87%E5%8A%BF&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">磁标势<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span></h3><p data-pid="svI7CWDs">注意使用情况，在没有自由电流的情况下用这个。即 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla\times H=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla\times H=0</span></span></span> （相当特殊，电场也没有）</p><p data-pid="1WzjSWRb">此时由于“梯无旋”，可以设 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="H"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">H</span></span></span> 为 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="H=-\nabla\phi_m"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">H=-\nabla\phi_m</span></span></span> , <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\phi_m"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\phi_m</span></span></span> 为磁标势</p><p data-pid="3RoxWXtg">用第一个积分变形， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\oint dl\cdot H=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\oint dl\cdot H=0</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="27SpPzyM">又有 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla\cdot B=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore
