data-eeimg="1" data-tex="Chapter \ \ 6"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">Chapter \ \ 6</span></span></span> 连续系统辐射</h2><h3>1.磁矢势 新标势</h3><p data-pid="X802M5xl"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="B=\nabla\times A"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">B=\nabla\times A</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla\times E=-\partial_t(\nabla\times A)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla\times E=-\partial_t(\nabla\times A)</span></span></span> 即 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla \times (E+\partial_t A)=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla \times (E+\partial_t A)=0</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="dfLj7D0d">引入标势 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="E+\partial_tA=-\nabla\phi"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">E+\partial_tA=-\nabla\phi</span></span></span> ，则 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="E=-\nabla\phi-\partial_tA"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">E=-\nabla\phi-\partial_tA</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="0p0nAdWT"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="A'=A+\nabla\psi"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">A'=A+\nabla\psi</span></span></span> , <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\phi'=\phi-\partial_t\psi"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\phi'=\phi-\partial_t\psi</span></span></span> 后电磁场不变</p><p data-pid="RKNbMhMf">所以要确定具体规范</p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>2.库伦规范</h3><p data-pid="20X85-gW"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla\cdot A=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla\cdot A=0</span></span></span> 所以 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla\cdot E=-\nabla^2\phi -0=\frac{\rho}{\epsilon_0}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla\cdot E=-\nabla^2\phi -0=\frac{\rho}{\epsilon_0}</span></span></span> （<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E5%BA%93%E4%BB%91%E5%9C%BA&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">库仑场<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>）</p><h3>3.<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E6%B4%9B%E4%BC%A6%E8%8C%A8%E8%A7%84%E8%8C%83&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">洛伦茨规范<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span></h3><p data-pid="qlYf7Xm6"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla\cdot A+\frac{1}{c^2}\partial_t\phi=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla\cdot A+\frac{1}{c^2}\partial_t\phi=0</span></span></span> </p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>4.达朗贝尔方程</h3><p data-pid="-duiq7PV"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="(\nabla^2-\frac{1}{c^2}\partial_t^2)A=\nabla(\nabla\cdot A+\frac{1}{c^2}\partial_t\phi)-\mu_0j"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">(\nabla^2-\frac{1}{c^2}\partial_t^2)A=\nabla(\nabla\cdot A+\frac{1}{c^2}\partial_t\phi)-\mu_0j</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="YKNTXHX6"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="(\nabla^2-\frac{1}{c^2}\partial_t^2)\phi=-\partial_t(\nabla\cdot A+\frac{1}{c^2}\partial_t\phi)-\frac{\rho}{\epsilon_0}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">(\nabla^2-\frac{1}{c^2}\partial_t^2)\phi=-\partial_t(\nabla\cdot A+\frac{1}{c^2}\partial_t\phi)-\frac{\rho}{\epsilon_0}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="oNuUN1lg">按洛伦兹规范则有</p><p data-pid="CeR9z5St"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="(\nabla^2-\frac{1}{c^2}\partial_t^2)A=-\mu_0j"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">(\nabla^2-\frac{1}{c^2}\partial_t^2)A=-\mu_0j</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="G8FzZdJa"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="(\nabla^2-\frac{1}{c^2}\partial_t^2)\phi=-\frac{\rho}{\epsilon_0}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">(\nabla^2-\frac{1}{c^2}\partial_t^2)\phi=-\frac{\rho}{\epsilon_0}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="DXt8TTvG">在自由空间情况：右边都是0</p><p data-pid="7V02cJy9"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="A=A_0e^{i(k\cdot x-\omega t)}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">A=A_0e^{i(k\cdot x-\omega t)}</span></span></span> <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\phi=\phi_0e^{i(k\cdot x-\omega t)}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\phi=\phi_0e^{i(k\cdot x-\omega t)}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="91MKU-lD">洛伦兹规范表示为 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="ik\cdot A+\frac{1}{c^2}(-i\omega)\phi=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">ik\cdot A+\frac{1}{c^2}(-i\omega)\phi=0</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="a34BhA6U"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="B=ik\times A"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">B=ik\times A</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="E=-i\frac{c^2}{\omega}k\times (k\times A)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">E=-i\frac{c^2}{\omega}k\times (k\times A)</span></span></span> </p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>5.<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E6%8E%A8%E8%BF%9F%E5%8A%BF&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">推迟势<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span></h3><p data-pid="Nihz_Iyb"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="(\nabla^2-\frac{1}{c^2}\partial_t^2)\phi=-\frac{\rho}{\epsilon_0}\to (\nabla^2-\frac{1}{c^2}\partial_t^2)\phi=-\frac{1}{\epsilon_0}Q(t)\delta^3(x)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">(\nabla^2-\frac{1}{c^2}\partial_t^2)\phi=-\frac{\rho}{\epsilon_0}\to (\nabla^2-\frac{1}{c^2}\partial_t^2)\phi=-\frac{1}{\epsilon_0}Q(t)\delta^3(x)</span></span></span></p><p data-pid="5FNLd4iu">改写为球坐标：解之得 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="u=f(t-\frac{r}{c})+g(t+\frac{r}{c})"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">u=f(t-\frac{r}{c})+g(t+\frac{r}{c})</span></span></span> （第二项从物理上看是诡异的 不如舍去）</p><p data-pid="-CQ70E91">得到 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\phi=\frac{1}{4\pi\epsilon_0}\frac{Q(t-\frac{r}{c})}{r}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\phi=\frac{1}{4\pi\epsilon_0}\frac{Q(t-\frac{r}{c})}{r}</span></span></span> ，把 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="r"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">r</span></span></span> 展开写（其实是 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="|x-x'|"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">|x-x'|</span></span></span> ）</p><p data-pid="f-22XdiH"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\phi(x,t)=\frac{1}{4\pi\epsilon_0}\int d^3x' \frac{\rho(x',t-\frac{|x-x'|}{c})}{|x-x'|}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\phi(x,t)=\frac{1}{4\pi\epsilon_0}\int d^3x' \frac{\rho(x',t-\frac{|x-x'|}{c})}{|x-x'|}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="kWbE569u"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="A(x,t)=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\int d^3x' \frac{j(x',t-\frac{|x-x'|}{c})}{|x-x'|}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">A(x,t)=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\int d^3x' \frac{j(x',t-\frac{|x-x'|}{c})}{|x-x'|}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="VFBu2T4S">由艰难的积分计算可知，洛伦兹规范和<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E7%94%B5%E8%8D%B7%E5%AE%88%E6%81%92&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">电荷守恒<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>是等价的</p><p data-pid="7AR3t21X">所以只要满足电荷守恒，洛伦兹规范下就能给出体系正确的电磁解</p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>6.小区域简谐电流矢势展开</h3><p data-pid="Sp9wrDDI"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="j(x,t)=j(x)e^{-i\omega t}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">j(x,t)=j(x)e^{-i\omega t}</span></span></span> ，则 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\rho"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\rho</span></span></span>也自动满足 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\omega"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\omega</span></span></span> 形式 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="-i\omega \rho+\nabla\cdot j=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">-i\omega \rho+\nabla\cdot j=0</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="Bj7VRDLI"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="A(x,t)=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\int d^3x' \frac{j(x')e^{-i\omega (t-\frac{|x-x'|}{c})}}{|x-x'|}=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\int d^3x' \frac{j(x')e^{i\omega (\frac{|x-x'|}{c})}}{|x-x'|}e^{-i\omega t}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">A(x,t)=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\int d^3x' \frac{j(x')e^{-i\omega (t-\frac{|x-x'|}{c})}}{|x-x'|}=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\int d^3x' \frac{j(x')e^{i\omega (\frac{|x-x'|}{c})}}{|x-x'|}e^{-i\omega t}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="dsVPCLrn"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="A(x)=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\int d^3x' \frac{j(x')e^{i\omega (\frac{r}{c})}}{r}=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\int d^3x' \frac{j(x')e^{ikr}}{r}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">A(x)=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\int d^3x' \frac{j(x')e^{i\omega (\frac{r}{c})}}{r}=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\int d^3x' \frac{j(x')e^{ikr}}{r}</span></span></span> ，再叠加时间的 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="-i\omega t"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">-i\omega t</span></span></span> 可以看出这应该是一个向外传播的球面波</p><p data-pid="uYKLX19C">那么接下来可以用 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="A"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">A</span></span></span> 求出 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="E"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">E</span></span></span> 与 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="B"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">B</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="83IJMy3R"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="B=\nabla\times A"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">B=\nabla\times A</span></span></span> ，先化简令 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="|x|=R&gt;&gt;\lambda"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">|x|=R&gt;&gt;\lambda</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="|x-x'|=R(1-x'\cdot x/R^2)=R-x'\cdot x/R"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">|x-x'|=R(1-x'\cdot x/R^2)=R-x'\cdot x/R</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="jGnHjGOa"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="A(x)=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\int d^3x' \frac{j(x')e^{ik(R-x'\cdot x/R)}}{R-x'\cdot x/R^2}=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\frac{e^{ikR}}{R}\int d^3 x'j(x')e^{-ikx'\cdot x/R}\frac{1}{1-x\cdot x'/R}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">A(x)=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\int d^3x' \frac{j(x')e^{ik(R-x'\cdot x/R)}}{R-x'\cdot x/R^2}=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\frac{e^{ikR}}{R}\int d^3 x'j(x')e^{-ikx'\cdot x/R}\frac{1}{1-x\cdot x'/R}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="73m8B1Iu">由于分母很接近1而且不影响相位，直接扔， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="e^x"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">e^x</span></span></span> 用 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="1+x"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">1+x</span></span></span> 近似</p><p data-pid="H_-asjxC"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="A(x)=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\frac{e^{ikR}}{R}\int d^3 x'j(x')(1-ikx'\cdot x/R)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">A(x)=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\frac{e^{ikR}}{R}\int d^3 x'j(x')(1-ikx'\cdot x/R)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="wFC78dkr">0级量 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="A_p=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\frac{e^{ikR}}{R}\int d^3 x' j(x',t)=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\frac{e^{ikR}}{R}\int d^3x' j\nabla\cdot x'\\=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\frac{e^{ikR}}{R}\int d^3x'(\nabla\cdot(jx')-(\nabla\cdot j)x')"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">A_p=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\frac{e^{ikR}}{R}\int d^3 x' j(x',t)=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\frac{e^{ikR}}{R}\int d^3x' j\nabla\cdot x'\\=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\frac{e^{ikR}}{R}\int d^3x'(\nabla\cdot(jx')-(\nabla\cdot j)x')</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="IGtv2lfp">第1项化面积分得0，第2项展开得到 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="A_p=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\frac{e^{ikR}}{R}\int d^3x'(-(\nabla\cdot j)x)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">A_p=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\frac{e^{ikR}}{R}\int d^3x'(-(\nabla\cdot j)x)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="hxSe_Uhj">代入电荷守恒 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="A_p=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\frac{e^{ikR}}{R}\int d^3x'(\partial_t \rho x')=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\frac{e^{ikR}}{R}\frac{d}{dt}\int d^3x'\rho(x',t)x'"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">A_p=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\frac{e^{ikR}}{R}\int d^3x'(\partial_t \rho x')=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\frac{e^{ikR}}{R}\frac{d}{dt}\int d^3x'\rho(x',t)x'</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="RcvTmXhP">即 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="A_p=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\frac{e^{ikR}}{R}\frac{dp(t)}{dt}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">A_p=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\frac{e^{ikR}}{R}\frac{dp(t)}{dt}</span></span></span> <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="A_pe^{-i\omega t}=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\frac{e^{i(kR-\omega t)}}{R}\frac{dp}{dt}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">A_pe^{-i\omega t}=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\frac{e^{i(kR-\omega t)}}{R}\frac{dp}{dt}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="hXLpq81K"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla\times A_p=B=ik\frac{x}{R}\times A_p"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla\times A_p=B=ik\frac{x}{R}\times A_p</span></span></span> （nabla为对x'求导） <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="n=\frac{\textbf{x}}{R}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">n=\frac{\textbf{x}}{R}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="R9E_YS-s">得到 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="cB_p=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}i\omega n\times\frac{e^{ikR}}{R}p_t=-\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}n\times \frac{e^{ikR}}{R}p_{tt}=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\frac{e^{ikR}}{R}p_{tt}\times n\\"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">cB_p=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}i\omega n\times\frac{e^{ikR}}{R}p_t=-\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}n\times \frac{e^{ikR}}{R}p_{tt}=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\frac{e^{ikR}}{R}p_{tt}\times n\\</span></span></span><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="E_p=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\frac{e^{ikR}}{R}(p_{tt}\times n)\times n\\"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">E_p=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\frac{e^{ikR}}{R}(p_{tt}\times n)\times n\\</span></span></span></p><p data-pid="-utCF-O5"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="B"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">B</span></span></span> 在 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\phi"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\phi</span></span></span> 方向， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="E"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">E</span></span></span> 在 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\theta"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\theta</span></span></span> 方向，</p><p data-pid="7Ktkyurj"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="cB_p=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\frac{e^{ikR}}{R}|p_{tt}|\sin\theta e_\phi"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">cB_p=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\frac{e^{ikR}}{R}|p_{tt}|\sin\theta e_\phi</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="E_p=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\frac{e^{ikR}}{R}|p_{tt}|\sin\theta e_\theta"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">E_p=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\frac{e^{ikR}}{R}|p_{tt}|\sin\theta e_\theta</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="2trnauh0">于是能流密度时间均值： <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="&lt;S&gt;=\frac{1}{2}R(E^* \times H)=\frac{|p_{tt}^2|}{32\pi^2\epsilon_0c^3R^2}\sin^2\theta \textbf{n}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">&lt;S&gt;=\frac{1}{2}R(E^* \times H)=\frac{|p_{tt}^2|}{32\pi^2\epsilon_0c^3R^2}\sin^2\theta \textbf{n}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="9_AEgmov">对球面积分(即 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\cdot \int_0^{2\pi}d\phi\int_0^{2\pi} \sin\theta d\theta"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\cdot \int_0^{2\pi}d\phi\int_0^{2\pi} \sin\theta d\theta</span></span></span> )最后得到 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="P=\frac{1}{4\pi\epsilon_0}\frac{|p_{tt}|}{3c^3}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">P=\frac{1}{4\pi\epsilon_0}\frac{|p_{tt}|}{3c^3}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="wtOtjsYY"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="p_t=\int_L Idl"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">p_t=\int_L Idl</span></span></span>  （也可以写成这个形式），再求导就再乘 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="-i\omega"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">-i\omega</span></span></span> </p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>7.<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E7%A3%81%E5%81%B6%E6%9E%81%E8%BE%90%E5%B0%84&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">磁偶极辐射<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>，电四极辐射</h3><p data-pid="YMrvFdT4">一阶项： <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="A_1=-ik\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\frac{e^{ikR}}{R}\int d^3 x'j(x')(x'\cdot n)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">A_1=-ik\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\frac{e^{ikR}}{R}\int d^3 x'j(x')(x'\cdot n)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="a5xooVbc">经过Hard Hard计算得到后面那个积分是 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\frac{1}{6}n\cdot D_t+m\times n"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\frac{1}{6}n\cdot D_t+m\times n</span></span></span> （D,m两个都是 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="e^{-i\omega t}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">e^{-i\omega t}</span></span></span> 形式）</p><p data-pid="E3Dsm9YV"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="A_1=-ik\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\frac{e^{ikR}}{R}(\frac{1}{6}n\cdot D_t+m\times n)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">A_1=-ik\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\frac{e^{ikR}}{R}(\frac{1}{6}n\cdot D_t+m\times n)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="oXjRplAJ">分开来看： <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="A_m=-ik\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\frac{e^{ikR}}{R}(m\times n)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">A_m=-ik\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\frac{e^{ikR}}{R}(m\times n)</span></span></span> ，一样是 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="B=ikn\times A，ikn\times B=i\omega E"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">B=ikn\times A，ikn\times B=i\omega E</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="nMjiiwHo"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="cB_m=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi c}\frac{e^{ikR}}{R}(m_{tt}\times n)\times n\\ E_m=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi c}\frac{e^{ikR}}{R}(m_{tt}\times n)\\"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">cB_m=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi c}\frac{e^{ikR}}{R}(m_{tt}\times n)\times n\\ E_m=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi c}\frac{e^{ikR}}{R}(m_{tt}\times n)\\</span></span></span></p><p data-pid="x-9nQ5Q3"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="&lt;S&gt;=\frac{1}{2}R(E^* \times H)=\frac{\mu_0\omega^4|m|^2}{32\pi^2\epsilon_0c^3R^2}\sin^2\theta \textbf{n}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">&lt;S&gt;=\frac{1}{2}R(E^* \times H)=\frac{\mu_0\omega^4|m|^2}{32\pi^2\epsilon_0c^3R^2}\sin^2\theta \textbf{n}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="R-I4smfu"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="P=\frac{\mu_0}{12\pi c^3}\omega^4|m|^2"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">P=\frac{\mu_0}{12\pi c^3}\omega^4|m|^2</span></span></span> （对应关系记一下）</p><p data-pid="vlooc_kI">另外一项： <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="A_D=-ik\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\frac{e^{ikR}}{R}(\frac{1}{6}n\cdot D_t)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">A_D=-ik\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\frac{e^{ikR}}{R}(\frac{1}{6}n\cdot D_t)</span></span></span> 令 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="d=n\cdot D"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">d=n\cdot D</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="A_D=-ik\frac{\mu_0}{24\pi}\frac{e^{ikR}}{R}d_t"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">A_D=-ik\frac{\mu_0}{24\pi}\frac{e^{ikR}}{R}d_t</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="P76X5h6Q">最后得到</p><p data-pid="DewAXvXq"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="cB_D=\frac{\mu_0}{24\pi c}\frac{e^{ikR}}{R}(d_{ttt}\times n)\\ E_D=\frac{\mu_0}{24\pi c}\frac{e^{ikR}}{R}(d_{ttt}\times n)\times n\\"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">cB_D=\frac{\mu_0}{24\pi c}\frac{e^{ikR}}{R}(d_{ttt}\times n)\\ E_D=\frac{\mu_0}{24\pi c}\frac{e^{ikR}}{R}(d_{ttt}\times n)\times n\\</span></span></span></p><p data-pid="wu-y_VTZ"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="&lt;S&gt;=\frac{cB^2\textbf{n}}{2\mu_0}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">&lt;S&gt;=\frac{cB^2\textbf{n}}{2\mu_0}</span></span></span> </p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><p data-pid="0mTz6M2G">总之辐射能量（幂次）电偶&gt;磁偶=电四</p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>8.基尔霍夫积分(这太难算了)</h3><p data-pid="BbPiwvPE">记一下形式 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\psi(x)=-\frac{1}{4\pi}\oint_\Sigma dS' \frac{1}{r}e^{ikr}n\cdot [\nabla_{x'}\psi(x')+(ik-\frac{1}{r})\frac{\textbf{r}}{r}\psi]"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\psi(x)=-\frac{1}{4\pi}\oint_\Sigma dS' \frac{1}{r}e^{ikr}n\cdot [\nabla_{x'}\psi(x')+(ik-\frac{1}{r})\frac{\textbf{r}}{r}\psi]</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="lMAdwhuN">夫琅禾费衍射化简： <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\psi(x)=-i\psi_0\frac{e^{ikR}}{4\pi R}\int_{S_0} dS'e^{i(k_1-k_2)\cdot x'}(k_1+k_2)\cdot n"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\psi(x)=-i\psi_0\frac{e^{ikR}}{4\pi R}\int_{S_0} dS'e^{i(k_1-k_2)\cdot x'}(k_1+k_2)\cdot n</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="z2XJq0t6"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\psi(x)=\psi(0)\frac{2kab}{\pi}\frac{e^{ikR}}{R}\frac{\sin(k_xa)}{k_x a}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\psi(x)=\psi(0)\frac{2kab}{\pi}\frac{e^{ikR}}{R}\frac{\sin(k_xa)}{k_x a}</span></span></span> 方孔衍射  , <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="k_x=k\sin \alpha"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">k_x=k\sin \alpha</span></span></span> </p><hr><h2><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="Chapter \ \ 7"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">Chapter \ \ 7</span></span></span> 运动粒子电磁辐射</h2><p data-pid="J--aZnpZ"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\phi(x,t)=\frac{1}{4\pi\epsilon_0}\int d^3 x'\frac{\rho(x',t-\frac{|x-x'|}{c})}{|x-x'|}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\phi(x,t)=\frac{1}{4\pi\epsilon_0}\int d^3 x'\frac{\rho(x',t-\frac{|x-x'|}{c})}{|x-x'|}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="OySWvoId"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="A(x,t)=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\int d^3 x'\frac{j(x',t-\frac{|x-x'|}{c})}{|x-x'|}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">A(x,t)=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\int d^3 x'\frac{j(x',t-\frac{|x-x'|}{c})}{|x-x'|}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="9rZI0_9C"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\phi(x,t)=\frac{1}{4\pi\epsilon_0}\frac{q}{L}\int_{L_e} dl\frac{1}{|x-x'|}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\phi(x,t)=\frac{1}{4\pi\epsilon_0}\frac{q}{L}\int_{L_e} dl\frac{1}{|x-x'|}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="OUBXsepw">积分长度为”伸长“的棒子</p><p data-pid="mXnl7bEC"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\Delta t=t_远-t_近=\frac{1}{c}L_e\cdot \frac{(x-x_e)}{|x-x_e|}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\Delta t=t_远-t_近=\frac{1}{c}L_e\cdot \frac{(x-x_e)}{|x-x_e|}</span></span></span> （ <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="x_e"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">x_e</span></span></span> 为 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="x_远"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">x_远</span></span></span> 和 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="x_近"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">x_近</span></span></span> 中点）</p><p data-pid="EVEKOBeq"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="L_e=L+v_e\Delta t"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">L_e=L+v_e\Delta t</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="L_e=L\frac{1}{1-\frac{v_e}{c}\cdot \frac{(x-x_c)}{|x-x_c|}}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">L_e=L\frac{1}{1-\frac{v_e}{c}\cdot \frac{(x-x_c)}{|x-x_c|}}</span></span></span> , <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\phi(x,t)=\frac{1}{4\pi\epsilon_0}\frac{q}{L}\frac{L}{(1-\frac{v_e}{c}\cdot(\frac{(x-x_e)}{|x-x_e|}))|x-x'|)}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\phi(x,t)=\frac{1}{4\pi\epsilon_0}\frac{q}{L}\frac{L}{(1-\frac{v_e}{c}\cdot(\frac{(x-x_e)}{|x-x_e|}))|x-x'|)}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="GSZNZMUZ">可以把 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="|x-x'|"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">|x-x'|</span></span></span> 近似为 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="|x-x_e|"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">|x-x_e|</span></span></span> ，得到 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\phi(x,t)=\frac{1}{4\pi\epsilon_0}\frac{q}{(|x-x_e|-\frac{v_e}{c}\cdot(x-x_e))}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\phi(x,t)=\frac{1}{4\pi\epsilon_0}\frac{q}{(|x-x_e|-\frac{v_e}{c}\cdot(x-x_e))}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="pPD252Tv">同理 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="A(x,t)=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\frac{qv_e}{(|x-x_e|-\frac{v_e}{c}\cdot(x-x_e))}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">A(x,t)=\frac{\mu_0}{4\pi}\frac{qv_e}{(|x-x_e|-\frac{v_e}{c}\cdot(x-x_e))}</span></span></span> (与物体形状无关)</p><p data-pid="r-Ap72mT">以上为李纳维谢尔势</p><p data-pid="yUcl8fOM"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="B(x,t)=\nabla \times A"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">B(x,t)=\nabla \times A</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="E(x,t)=-\nabla\phi-\partial_t A"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">E(x,t)=-\nabla\phi-\partial_t A</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="jU0IrUYB">因为 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="c(t-t')=|x-x_e(t')|"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">c(t-t')=|x-x_e(t')|</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="_y8ar_aU"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\partial_t t'(x,t)=\frac{1}{1-v_e\cdot n/c}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\partial_t t'(x,t)=\frac{1}{1-v_e\cdot n/c}</span></span></span>  , <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla t'(x,t)=-\frac{\textbf{n}}{c}\frac{1}{1-v_e\cdot n/c}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla t'(x,t)=-\frac{\textbf{n}}{c}\frac{1}{1-v_e\cdot n/c}</span></span></span> </p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>1.低速情况</h3><p data-pid="_EpLJUvx"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\partial_t t'=1"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\partial_t t'=1</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nabla t'=-\frac{\textbf{n}}{c}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nabla t'=-\frac{\textbf{n}}{c}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="0DjgdZt3"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="B=\nabla_x \times A=\nabla \times A(x,t')|_{t'=C}+\nabla t'\times \partial_{t'}A(x,t')"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">B=\nabla_x \times A=\nabla \times A(x,t')|_{t'=C}+\nabla t'\times \partial_{t'}A(x,t')</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="8FT0B3QT"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="B=\frac{Zev_e\times r}{4\pi\epsilon_0c^2r^3}+\frac{Zea_e\times r}{4\pi\epsilon_0 c^3 r^2}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">B=\frac{Zev_e\times r}{4\pi\epsilon_0c^2r^3}+\frac{Zea_e\times r}{4\pi\epsilon_0 c^3 r^2}</span></span></span> ，第二项为辐射电场</p><p data-pid="QxtrHYjZ"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="E=-\nabla\phi(x,t)-\partial_t A(x,t)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">E=-\nabla\phi(x,t)-\partial_t A(x,t)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="HDBtAGQi"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="E=\frac{Ze\textbf{r}}{4\pi\epsilon_0r^3}+\frac{Ze}{4\pi\epsilon_0c^2r^3}r\times(r\times a_e)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">E=\frac{Ze\textbf{r}}{4\pi\epsilon_0r^3}+\frac{Ze}{4\pi\epsilon_0c^2r^3}r\times(r\times a_e)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="0vEjW5UK">B,E辐射电场均与加速度成正比</p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>2.<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E7%94%B5%E5%81%B6%E6%9E%81%E8%BE%90%E5%B0%84&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">电偶极辐射<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 data-tex="E=\frac{Ze}{4\pi\epsilon_0c^2r^3}r\times(r\times a_e)=\frac{1}{4\pi\epsilon_0c^2r^3}r\times(r\times (p_e(t'))_{tt})"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E7%AE%80%E8%B0%90%E8%BF%90%E5%8A%A8&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">简谐运动<svg
