2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="x\sim e^{-i\omega t}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">x\sim e^{-i\omega t}</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="E=\frac{1}{4\pi\epsilon_0c^2r^3}r\times(r\times (p_e(0))_{tt})e^{i(k\cdot r-\omega t)}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">E=\frac{1}{4\pi\epsilon_0c^2r^3}r\times(r\times (p_e(0))_{tt})e^{i(k\cdot r-\omega t)}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="EHq3HKj3"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="r_e=\frac{e^2}{4\pi \epsilon_0 m_ec^2}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">r_e=\frac{e^2}{4\pi \epsilon_0 m_ec^2}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="5k1tnXdb"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="S=Z^2\frac{r_e}{c}m_e|a_e|^2\frac{1}{4\pi r^2}\sin^2 \theta \textbf{n}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">S=Z^2\frac{r_e}{c}m_e|a_e|^2\frac{1}{4\pi r^2}\sin^2 \theta \textbf{n}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="TgwGCESV"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="E=Z\frac{m_e}{e}\frac{r_e}{r}n\times (n\times a_e)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">E=Z\frac{m_e}{e}\frac{r_e}{r}n\times (n\times a_e)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="iwxTVP62"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="P=\frac{2}{3}Z^2 \frac{r_e}{c}m_ea_e^2"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">P=\frac{2}{3}Z^2 \frac{r_e}{c}m_ea_e^2</span></span></span> （全记住）</p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>3.塞曼效应</h3><p data-pid="JTiBLF0C"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\omega_c=\frac{eB}{m_0}&lt;&lt;\omega_0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\omega_c=\frac{eB}{m_0}&lt;&lt;\omega_0</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="NWQAZtdI"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="r=r_ze_ze^{-i\omega_0 t}+r_{+}(e_x-ie_y)e^{-i(\omega_0+\frac{1}{2}\omega_c)t}+r_{-}(e_x-ie_y)e^{-i(\omega_0-\frac{1}{2}\omega_c)t}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">r=r_ze_ze^{-i\omega_0 t}+r_{+}(e_x-ie_y)e^{-i(\omega_0+\frac{1}{2}\omega_c)t}+r_{-}(e_x-ie_y)e^{-i(\omega_0-\frac{1}{2}\omega_c)t}</span></span></span> </p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>4.特殊辐射</h3><p data-pid="dAlfinP5">1.加速度平行速度</p><p data-pid="IzQ3eDMY"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="P=\frac{2}{3}Z^2 \frac{r_e}{c}m_ea_e^2\gamma^6"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">P=\frac{2}{3}Z^2 \frac{r_e}{c}m_ea_e^2\gamma^6</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="fZ77uaYv">2.加速度垂直速度</p><p data-pid="jdDcXZqU"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="P=\frac{2}{3}Z^2 \frac{r_e}{c}m_ea_e^2\gamma^4"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">P=\frac{2}{3}Z^2 \frac{r_e}{c}m_ea_e^2\gamma^4</span></span></span> </p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>5.匀速直线运动粒子</h3><p data-pid="IBJrMZG2"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="A_x=\gamma\beta\frac{1}{4\pi\epsilon_0 c}\frac{q}{\sqrt{[\gamma(x-vt)]^2+y^2+z^2}}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">A_x=\gamma\beta\frac{1}{4\pi\epsilon_0 c}\frac{q}{\sqrt{[\gamma(x-vt)]^2+y^2+z^2}}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="laGkudof"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="A_y=A_z=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">A_y=A_z=0</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\frac{\phi}{c}=\gamma\frac{1}{4\pi\epsilon_0 c}\frac{q}{\sqrt{[\gamma(x-vt)]^2+y^2+z^2}}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\frac{\phi}{c}=\gamma\frac{1}{4\pi\epsilon_0 c}\frac{q}{\sqrt{[\gamma(x-vt)]^2+y^2+z^2}}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="9e7r7sb9">观察产生的电场： <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="E=\frac{\gamma}{[1+\gamma^2(\frac{v}{c}\cdot n)^2]^{3/2}}E_{normal}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">E=\frac{\gamma}{[1+\gamma^2(\frac{v}{c}\cdot n)^2]^{3/2}}E_{normal}</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="B=\frac{v}{c^2}\times E"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">B=\frac{v}{c^2}\times E</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="rP1a4Iaj">所以平行速度方向电场为 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="E=\frac{E_{normal}}{\gamma^2}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">E=\frac{E_{normal}}{\gamma^2}</span></span></span> ，垂直为 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="E_{\bot}=\gamma E_{normal}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">E_{\bot}=\gamma E_{normal}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="140cRPxK">（所以从电场来看第九章的有一题 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\frac{F_{\bot总}}{q}=E_\bot+v\times B=(1-\beta^2)E=\gamma (1-\beta^2)E_{normal}=\frac{1}{\gamma}E_{normal}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\frac{F_{\bot总}}{q}=E_\bot+v\times B=(1-\beta^2)E=\gamma (1-\beta^2)E_{normal}=\frac{1}{\gamma}E_{normal}</span></span></span> ）</p><h3>6.切伦科夫辐射角度</h3><p data-pid="ZlzqL8bZ"><span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E5%8D%8A%E9%A1%B6%E8%A7%92&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">半顶角<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span> <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\sin\alpha=\frac{\frac{c}{n}}{v}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\sin\alpha=\frac{\frac{c}{n}}{v}</span></span></span> ，即速度比介质光速快，所以出现锥面波</p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><hr><h2><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="Chapter \ \ 8"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">Chapter \ \ 8</span></span></span> 散射吸收</h2><p data-pid="y86iN7WB"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="E=\frac{\gamma}{[1+\gamma^2(\frac{v}{c}\cdot n)^2]^{3/2}}E_{normal}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">E=\frac{\gamma}{[1+\gamma^2(\frac{v}{c}\cdot n)^2]^{3/2}}E_{normal}</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="B=\frac{v}{c^2}\times E"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">B=\frac{v}{c^2}\times E</span></span></span></p><p data-pid="EQLQkrRo">匀速直线运动电子携带动量 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="p=\frac{1}{6}\gamma \frac{e^2}{\pi\epsilon_0c^2 a}v"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">p=\frac{1}{6}\gamma \frac{e^2}{\pi\epsilon_0c^2 a}v</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="e3pLXd8d"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="W_{elec}=\frac{e^2}{8\pi \epsilon_0 a}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">W_{elec}=\frac{e^2}{8\pi \epsilon_0 a}</span></span></span> </p><h3>1.辐射 简谐运动阻尼</h3><p data-pid="Xtqlrc0U"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="ma=F_{ext}+F_s"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">ma=F_{ext}+F_s</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="F_s=\frac{2}{3}Z^2\frac{r_e}{c}m_e v_{tt}（急动度）"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">F_s=\frac{2}{3}Z^2\frac{r_e}{c}m_e v_{tt}（急动度）</span></span></span> （辐射阻力）</p><p data-pid="JnnDLwfx">运动方程 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="x_{tt}+\omega_0^2x=\frac{2}{3}\frac{r_e}{c}x_{ttt}=-\frac{2}{3}\frac{r_e}{c}\omega_0^2x_t"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">x_{tt}+\omega_0^2x=\frac{2}{3}\frac{r_e}{c}x_{ttt}=-\frac{2}{3}\frac{r_e}{c}\omega_0^2x_t</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="0ZbHeSKl">得到阻尼系数 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\gamma=\frac{2}{3}\frac{r_e}{c}\omega_0^2"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\gamma=\frac{2}{3}\frac{r_e}{c}\omega_0^2</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="OKDsCW9v"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="x_{tt}+\omega_0^2x+\gamma x_t=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">x_{tt}+\omega_0^2x+\gamma x_t=0</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="_hy77LPW">对于弱阻尼 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\omega=\omega_0-i\frac{\gamma}{2}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\omega=\omega_0-i\frac{\gamma}{2}</span></span></span> , <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="x=x_0e^{-\gamma/2 t}e^{-i\omega_0 t}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">x=x_0e^{-\gamma/2 t}e^{-i\omega_0 t}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="sUJ4kSrJ"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="W=A\frac{\gamma/2\pi}{(\omega-\omega_0)^2+(\gamma/2)^2}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">W=A\frac{\gamma/2\pi}{(\omega-\omega_0)^2+(\gamma/2)^2}</span></span></span> </p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>2.自由 受辐射电子</h3><p data-pid="rmkW1zCE"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="x_{tt}+\gamma x_t=-\frac{e}{m_e}E_0e^{-i\omega t}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">x_{tt}+\gamma x_t=-\frac{e}{m_e}E_0e^{-i\omega t}</span></span></span>  求导变 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="-i\omega"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">-i\omega</span></span></span> 即可</p><p data-pid="v1lFgqdM"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="x=\frac{1}{\omega^2+i\gamma\omega}\frac{e}{m_e}E_0e^{-i\omega t}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">x=\frac{1}{\omega^2+i\gamma\omega}\frac{e}{m_e}E_0e^{-i\omega t}</span></span></span> ，一般有 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\gamma&lt;&lt;\omega"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\gamma&lt;&lt;\omega</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="3ACuqTyW"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="E=Z\frac{m_e}{e}\frac{r_e}{r}n\times (n\times a_e)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">E=Z\frac{m_e}{e}\frac{r_e}{r}n\times (n\times a_e)</span></span></span></p><p data-pid="S92OJzgW">取 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="Z=-1"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">Z=-1</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="&lt;S&gt;_t=\frac{1}{2}\epsilon_0E_0^2c\frac{r_e^2}{r^2}\sin^2\alpha"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">&lt;S&gt;_t=\frac{1}{2}\epsilon_0E_0^2c\frac{r_e^2}{r^2}\sin^2\alpha</span></span></span> , <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="&lt;I_入&gt;=\frac{1}{2}\epsilon_0E_0^2 c"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">&lt;I_入&gt;=\frac{1}{2}\epsilon_0E_0^2 c</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="_68f9s8o"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="&lt;S&gt;_t=I\frac{r_e^2}{r^2}\sin^2\theta"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">&lt;S&gt;_t=I\frac{r_e^2}{r^2}\sin^2\theta</span></span></span> 立体角积分得到，( 乘<span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="2\pi\sin\theta"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">2\pi\sin\theta</span></span></span> 从0到 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\pi"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\pi</span></span></span> 积分)</p><p data-pid="zOttYDYE"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="P=\frac{8\pi}{3}r_e^2I_0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">P=\frac{8\pi}{3}r_e^2I_0</span></span></span> ，得到等效面积 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\sigma=\frac{8\pi}{3}r_e^2"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\sigma=\frac{8\pi}{3}r_e^2</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="-5EnZqLE">非偏振情况： <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\bar S=\frac{1+\cos^2\theta}{2}\frac{r_e^2}{r^2}I_0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\bar S=\frac{1+\cos^2\theta}{2}\frac{r_e^2}{r^2}I_0</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="-YdYSUUY"><span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E5%BE%AE%E5%88%86%E6%88%AA%E9%9D%A2&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">微分截面<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>为 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\frac{d\sigma}{d\Omega}=\frac{1+\cos^2\theta}{2}r_e^2"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\frac{d\sigma}{d\Omega}=\frac{1+\cos^2\theta}{2}r_e^2</span></span></span> </p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>3.简谐受<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E8%BE%90%E5%B0%84%E9%98%BB%E5%B0%BC&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">辐射阻尼<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>粒子</h3><p data-pid="4McnPZFB"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="x_{tt}+\gamma x_t+\omega_0^2x=-\frac{e}{m_e}E_0e^{-i\omega t}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">x_{tt}+\gamma x_t+\omega_0^2x=-\frac{e}{m_e}E_0e^{-i\omega t}</span></span></span> ,还是 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="-i\omega"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">-i\omega</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="tN3_LLY7"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="&lt;S&gt;_t=\frac{1}{2}\epsilon_0E_0^2c\frac{r_e^2}{r^2}\sin^2\alpha\cdot \frac{\omega^4}{(\omega_0^2-\omega^2)^2+\gamma^2\omega^2}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">&lt;S&gt;_t=\frac{1}{2}\epsilon_0E_0^2c\frac{r_e^2}{r^2}\sin^2\alpha\cdot \frac{\omega^4}{(\omega_0^2-\omega^2)^2+\gamma^2\omega^2}</span></span></span> (这里还没舍掉阻尼，所以看上去和上面不一样)</p><p data-pid="l7b3PbDu"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="P=\frac{8\pi}{3}r_e^2I_0\cdot \frac{\omega^4}{(\omega_0^2-\omega^2)^2+\gamma^2\omega^2}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">P=\frac{8\pi}{3}r_e^2I_0\cdot \frac{\omega^4}{(\omega_0^2-\omega^2)^2+\gamma^2\omega^2}</span></span></span> , <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\sigma=\frac{8\pi}{3}r_e^2\cdot \frac{\omega^4}{(\omega_0^2-\omega^2)^2+\gamma^2\omega^2}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\sigma=\frac{8\pi}{3}r_e^2\cdot \frac{\omega^4}{(\omega_0^2-\omega^2)^2+\gamma^2\omega^2}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="S8yuBns5">1. <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\omega&lt;&lt;\omega_0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\omega&lt;&lt;\omega_0</span></span></span> <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\sigma=\frac{8\pi}{3}r_e^2\cdot \frac{\omega^4}{\omega_0^4}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\sigma=\frac{8\pi}{3}r_e^2\cdot \frac{\omega^4}{\omega_0^4}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="mC_vui6i">2. <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\omega=\omega_0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\omega=\omega_0</span></span></span> <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\sigma=\frac{8\pi}{3}r_e^2 \frac{\omega^2}{\gamma^2}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\sigma=\frac{8\pi}{3}r_e^2 \frac{\omega^2}{\gamma^2}</span></span></span> （共振 面积急剧增大，辐射急剧上升）</p><p data-pid="3VpBU8NA">3. <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\omega&gt;&gt;\omega_0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\omega&gt;&gt;\omega_0</span></span></span> <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\sigma=\frac{8\pi}{3}r_e^2\cdot\frac{\omega^4}{\omega_0^4}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\sigma=\frac{8\pi}{3}r_e^2\cdot\frac{\omega^4}{\omega_0^4}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="Nl4-Tj6D">共振时辐射频率约为 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="P=\frac{8\pi}{3}r_e^2\int_0^{+\infty} I_0(\omega)\cdot \frac{\omega^4}{(\omega_0^2-\omega^2)^2+\gamma^2\omega^2}d\omega=\frac{4\pi^2}{3}r_e^2I_0(\omega_0)\frac{\omega_0^2}{\gamma}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">P=\frac{8\pi}{3}r_e^2\int_0^{+\infty} I_0(\omega)\cdot \frac{\omega^4}{(\omega_0^2-\omega^2)^2+\gamma^2\omega^2}d\omega=\frac{4\pi^2}{3}r_e^2I_0(\omega_0)\frac{\omega_0^2}{\gamma}</span></span></span> </p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>4.吸收模型</h3><p data-pid="tzK70QA_"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="P=-nex"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">P=-nex</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="xgdjde_y">则 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="P=\omega_{pe}^2\frac{1}{\omega_0^2-\omega^2-i\gamma\omega}\epsilon _0E"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">P=\omega_{pe}^2\frac{1}{\omega_0^2-\omega^2-i\gamma\omega}\epsilon _0E</span></span></span> <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="D=\epsilon_0 E+P"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">D=\epsilon_0 E+P</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="vTfdoy7d">实部色散，虚部吸收</p><p data-pid="hStxqnHK"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="R(\epsilon_r)=1+\omega_{pe}^2\frac{\omega_0^2-\omega^2}{(\omega_0^2-\omega^2)^2+\gamma^2\omega^2}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">R(\epsilon_r)=1+\omega_{pe}^2\frac{\omega_0^2-\omega^2}{(\omega_0^2-\omega^2)^2+\gamma^2\omega^2}</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="I(\epsilon_r)=\omega_{pe}^2\frac{\gamma\omega}{(\omega_0^2-\omega^2)^2+\gamma^2\omega^2}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">I(\epsilon_r)=\omega_{pe}^2\frac{\gamma\omega}{(\omega_0^2-\omega^2)^2+\gamma^2\omega^2}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="OVk4-F9T"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="c^2k^2=\omega^2(R+iI)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">c^2k^2=\omega^2(R+iI)</span></span></span> ，折射率取实部 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\sqrt{R}=(1+\omega_{pe}^2\frac{\omega_0^2-\omega^2}{(\omega_0^2-\omega^2)^2+\gamma^2\omega^2})^{1/2}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\sqrt{R}=(1+\omega_{pe}^2\frac{\omega_0^2-\omega^2}{(\omega_0^2-\omega^2)^2+\gamma^2\omega^2})^{1/2}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="9U3f1LRF">一般有 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\omega&lt;&lt;\omega_0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\omega&lt;&lt;\omega_0</span></span></span> （因为 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\omega"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\omega</span></span></span> 一般在可见光）, <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\omega_0&gt;\omega_{pe}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\omega_0&gt;\omega_{pe}</span></span></span> 不然黑障了（</p><p data-pid="BZ6BLkDN">近似得到 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="n=1+\frac{1}{2}\frac{\omega_{pe}^2}{\omega_0^2}(1+\frac{\omega^2}{\omega_0^2})"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">n=1+\frac{1}{2}\frac{\omega_{pe}^2}{\omega_0^2}(1+\frac{\omega^2}{\omega_0^2})</span></span></span> 所以频率高对于高折射率</p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>5.黑体辐射</h3><p data-pid="8LcTLIDb"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\frac{4\pi^2}{3}r_e^2I_0(\omega_0)\frac{\omega_0^2}{\gamma}=3\gamma kT"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\frac{4\pi^2}{3}r_e^2I_0(\omega_0)\frac{\omega_0^2}{\gamma}=3\gamma kT</span></span></span> (辐射阻尼率)</p><p data-pid="nMsowGk_"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="I_0(\omega)=\frac{\omega^2}{\pi^2c^2}kT"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">I_0(\omega)=\frac{\omega^2}{\pi^2c^2}kT</span></span></span> （瑞利）</p><p data-pid="Zep_a6xk">普朗克：能量量子化 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="n\hbar\omega"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">n\hbar\omega</span></span></span> 配分函数 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\to"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\to</span></span></span> <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="I_0(\omega)=\frac{\omega^2}{\pi^2c^2}\frac{\hbar \omega}{e^{\frac{\hbar \omega}{kT}}-1}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">I_0(\omega)=\frac{\omega^2}{\pi^2c^2}\frac{\hbar \omega}{e^{\frac{\hbar \omega}{kT}}-1}</span></span></span> </p><hr><h2><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="Chapter \ \ 9"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">Chapter \ \ 9</span></span></span> 狭义相对论</h2><h3>1.两条原理</h3><p data-pid="EYY0GUEC">相对性原理：物理规律在所有惯性系不变</p><p data-pid="M9GLPy9y">光速不变：自由空间光速不依赖光源的运动速度</p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>2.时间间隔</h3><p data-pid="NL2Ay05e"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="x^2+y^2+z^2-c^2t^2=0，x'^2+y'^2+z'^2-c^2t'^2=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">x^2+y^2+z^2-c^2t^2=0，x'^2+y'^2+z'^2-c^2t'^2=0</span></span></span> (光源移动时候满足的方程)</p><p data-pid="-YRtRiAn"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\Delta s^2=|\Delta x|^2-c^2\Delta t^2"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\Delta s^2=|\Delta x|^2-c^2\Delta t^2</span></span></span> </p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>3.<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E6%B4%9B%E4%BC%A6%E5%85%B9%E5%8F%98%E6%8D%A2&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">洛伦兹变换<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span></h3><p data-pid="F3-gv7gw">通过相对速度反复换系，证明了 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\Delta s'^2=\Delta s^2"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\Delta s'^2=\Delta s^2</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="vaFDFV78"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="x'=\gamma(x-\beta ct)\\ y'=y\\ z'=z\\ ct'=\gamma(ct-\beta x)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">x'=\gamma(x-\beta ct)\\ y'=y\\ z'=z\\ ct'=\gamma(ct-\beta x)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="JlFqHHWv"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="x=\gamma(x'+\beta ct')\\ y=y'\\ z=z'\\ ct=\gamma(ct'+\beta x')"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">x=\gamma(x'+\beta ct')\\ y=y'\\ z=z'\\ ct=\gamma(ct'+\beta x')</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="k2slWC41">('为动系 无'为静止系)</p><p data-pid="_BoasEjr">对洛伦兹变换求微分即可得知</p><p data-pid="-hhDWHoZ"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="u_x'=\frac{u_x-v}{1-\frac{vu_x}{c^2}}\\ u_y'=\frac{u_y/\gamma}{1-\frac{vu_x}{c^2}}\\ u_z'=\frac{u_z/\gamma}{1-\frac{vu_x}{c^2}}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">u_x'=\frac{u_x-v}{1-\frac{vu_x}{c^2}}\\ u_y'=\frac{u_y/\gamma}{1-\frac{vu_x}{c^2}}\\ u_z'=\frac{u_z/\gamma}{1-\frac{vu_x}{c^2}}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="FA0K24J3">重点是想像三个“意象”：静止系，在动的物体，在运动的系，变换就是已知一个系里的运动信息，求另外一个系应该得到的运动信息</p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>4.时空分类</h3><p data-pid="3kTv_TSu">发生先后顺序在惯性系变换中不变。</p><p data-pid="2llUcWQ9"><span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E7%B1%BB%E7%A9%BA%E9%97%B4%E9%9A%94&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">类空间隔<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>： <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\Delta s^2&gt;0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\Delta s^2&gt;0</span></span></span> 异地是绝对的</p><p data-pid="PhLzJWJo">类光间隔： <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\Delta s^2=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\Delta s^2=0</span></span></span> 光联系两个事件</p><p data-pid="HOS4fdHp">类时间隔： <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\Delta s^2&lt;0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\Delta s^2&lt;0</span></span></span> 不同时是绝对的</p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>5.动钟变慢/动尺变短</h3><p data-pid="1OyfcEk6">1.动钟变慢</p><p data-pid="H2v2jtVo">人在静止系看变化物，不动 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\Delta x=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\Delta x=0</span></span></span> ，已知这个信息，算在动系里的人应该看到的时间流逝</p><p data-pid="E-w14QCm"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="c\Delta t'=\gamma(c\Delta t-\beta \Delta x)=\gamma c\Delta t"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">c\Delta t'=\gamma(c\Delta t-\beta \Delta x)=\gamma c\Delta t</span></span></span> ，所以在动系里时间经过了 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\gamma"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\gamma</span></span></span> 倍。动系里的人看静止系的钟走的比动系慢</p><p data-pid="aCbaxTTm">反过来看要是已知动系里一个 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\Delta x'=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\Delta x'=0</span></span></span> 的物体，静止系也会觉得动系的钟慢了。 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="c\Delta t=\gamma(c\Delta t'+\beta \Delta x')=\gamma c\Delta t'"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">c\Delta t=\gamma(c\Delta t'+\beta \Delta x')=\gamma c\Delta t'</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="Iysm8FxC">例子：粒子衰变 固有寿命在和它一起动的动系里，在静系变大为 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\gamma"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\gamma</span></span></span> 倍，人在静止系测到寿命，所以 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\tau_固=t/\gamma"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\tau_固=t/\gamma</span></span></span> </p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><p data-pid="qpQahRqE">2.动尺变短</p><p data-pid="qPYn-2J7">在动系里测量一个相对静止物体长度为 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="l_0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">l_0</span></span></span> , 然后静止系测量：那得是同时（静止的人认为的）测起终点， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\Delta t=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\Delta t=0</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="sWO1EXrb"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\Delta x'=\gamma (\Delta x-\beta c\Delta t)=l_0=\gamma l_测"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\Delta x'=\gamma (\Delta x-\beta c\Delta t)=l_0=\gamma l_测</span></span></span> （因为 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\Delta l_测"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\Delta l_测</span></span></span> 被认为是 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\Delta x"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\Delta x</span></span></span> ）， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="l_测"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">l_测</span></span></span> 比 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="l_静=l_0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">l_静=l_0</span></span></span> 短</p><p data-pid="TS2wRTkT">反过来也一样，在动系里的人认为同时的测量 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\Delta t'=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\Delta t'=0</span></span></span> ，静止系的 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\Delta x=l_0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\Delta x=l_0</span></span></span> <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\Delta  x'"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\Delta  x'</span></span></span> 还是比 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="l_0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">l_0</span></span></span> 小</p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><p data-pid="O_xXJA-c">例子：长度 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="l_0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">l_0</span></span></span> 车厢，速度 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="v"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">v</span></span></span> 相对地运动，小球相对车厢速度 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="u_0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">u_0</span></span></span> 到首端，算<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E5%9C%B0%E9%9D%A2%E7%B3%BB&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">地面系<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>看见耗时</p><p data-pid="1SdZbD0k"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="c\Delta t=\gamma (c\Delta t'+\beta \Delta x')"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">c\Delta t=\gamma (c\Delta t'+\beta \Delta x')</span></span></span> 把动系令为速度v的系， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\Delta t'=\frac{l_0}{u_0}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\Delta t'=\frac{l_0}{u_0}</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\Delta x'=l_0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\Delta x'=l_0</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="j9e84-8f"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\Delta t=\gamma(\frac{l_0}{u_0}+\frac{v}{c^2}l_0)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\Delta t=\gamma(\frac{l_0}{u_0}+\frac{v}{c^2}l_0)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="qjTed78Y">不建议拿尺缩钟慢捯饬，这error rate太高了</p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>6.四维标量矢量张量</h3><p data-pid="_g04Qjch"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="x^\mu=(\textbf{x},ct)^T"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">x^\mu=(\textbf{x},ct)^T</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="Jgfhb1pm">三维情况： <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="dx'^i=\frac{\partial x'^i}{\partial x^j}dx^j=a_j^idx^j"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">dx'^i=\frac{\partial x'^i}{\partial x^j}dx^j=a_j^idx^j</span></span></span> (上下指标相消）</p><p data-pid="9Ik166YU"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="dx^i=\frac{\partial x^i}{\partial x'^j}dx'^j=b_j^idx'^j"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">dx^i=\frac{\partial x^i}{\partial x'^j}dx'^j=b_j^idx'^j</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="a_m^ib^m_j=\delta^i_j"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">a_m^ib^m_j=\delta^i_j</span></span></span> ，可理解为矩阵 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="AB=I"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">AB=I</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="NjNuq1eq">列向量一般为上指标（<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E5%8F%B3%E7%9F%A2&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">右矢<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>） <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="|dx'&gt;=A|dx&gt;"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">|dx'&gt;=A|dx&gt;</span></span></span> (<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E9%80%86%E5%8F%98&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">逆变<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>)A（ <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="a_j^i"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">a_j^i</span></span></span> ）为逆变原变换</p><p data-pid="LaGF_JEx"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="&lt;\partial'|=&lt;\partial|A^{-1}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">&lt;\partial'|=&lt;\partial|A^{-1}</span></span></span> （<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E5%8D%8F%E5%8F%98&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">协变<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>）B( <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="b^i_j"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">b^i_j</span></span></span> )为协变原变换</p><p data-pid="JDIh4VpQ">标量 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="d\phi=&lt;\partial|\phi|dx&gt;=&lt;\partial'|\phi'|dx'&gt;"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">d\phi=&lt;\partial|\phi|dx&gt;=&lt;\partial'|\phi'|dx'&gt;</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="4YGkp2wO">遵循逆变原变换的 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="F'^i=AF^j"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">F'^i=AF^j</span></span></span> 为逆变矢量， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="F'_i=F_jB"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">F'_i=F_jB</span></span></span> 为<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E5%8D%8F%E5%8F%98%E7%9F%A2%E9%87%8F&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">协变矢量<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span></p><p data-pid="S2zITNlO">两次逆变：逆变张量 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="P'^{ij}=a^i_ka^j_lP^{kl}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">P'^{ij}=a^i_ka^j_lP^{kl}</span></span></span> <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="|&gt;P|&gt;"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">|&gt;P|&gt;</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="MfyKxKvp">两次协变：<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E5%8D%8F%E5%8F%98%E5%BC%A0%E9%87%8F&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">协变张量<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span> <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="P_{ij}'=a_i^ka_j^lP_{kl}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">P_{ij}'=a_i^ka_j^lP_{kl}</span></span></span> <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="&lt;|P&lt;|"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">&lt;|P&lt;|</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="_umTJ2Rm">各有一次:<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E6%B7%B7%E5%90%88%E5%BC%A0%E9%87%8F&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">混合张量<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>     <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="&gt;|P|&lt;"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">&gt;|P|&lt;</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="VbUvUMti">度规 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="ds^2=g_{ij}dx^idx^j"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">ds^2=g_{ij}dx^idx^j</span></span></span> , <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="g_{ij}=\frac{\partial x}{\partial x^i}\frac{\partial x}{\partial x^j}+\frac{\partial y}{\partial x^i}\frac{\partial y}{\partial x^j}+\frac{\partial z}{\partial x^i}\frac{\partial z}{\partial x^j}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">g_{ij}=\frac{\partial x}{\partial x^i}\frac{\partial x}{\partial x^j}+\frac{\partial y}{\partial x^i}\frac{\partial y}{\partial x^j}+\frac{\partial z}{\partial x^i}\frac{\partial z}{\partial x^j}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="e_yyKwaR"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="ds^2=dx_idx^j"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">ds^2=dx_idx^j</span></span></span> , <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="g_{ij}dx^j=dx_i"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">g_{ij}dx^j=dx_i</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="v3zIUrli">（希腊字母取值1~4 拉丁字母取1~3）</p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>7.<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E6%B4%9B%E4%BC%A6%E5%85%B9%E7%A9%BA%E9%97%B4&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">洛伦兹空间<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>四维标量矢量张量</h3><p data-pid="_OSAyEgs">洛伦兹变换： <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="x'^\mu=a_\nu^\mu x^\nu"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">x'^\mu=a_\nu^\mu x^\nu</span></span></span> a上标为行标，下标为列标</p><p data-pid="S1twQzag"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\matrix{\gamma \ 0\  0 -\gamma\beta\\0\ 1\ 0\ 0\\0\ 0\ 1\ 0\\-\gamma\beta\  0\ 0\ \gamma}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\matrix{\gamma \ 0\  0 -\gamma\beta\\0\ 1\ 0\ 0\\0\ 0\ 1\ 0\\-\gamma\beta\  0\ 0\ \gamma}</span></span></span> （实在不会打矩阵）</p><p data-pid="USVeMgj_">逆变换： <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="x^\mu=a_\mu^\nu x'^\nu"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">x^\mu=a_\mu^\nu x'^\nu</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="XsXPYjZ5"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\matrix{\gamma \ 0\  0 +\gamma\beta\\0\ 1\ 0\ 0\\0\ 0\ 1\ 0\\+\gamma\beta\  0\ 0\ \gamma}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\matrix{\gamma \ 0\  0 +\gamma\beta\\0\ 1\ 0\ 0\\0\ 0\ 1\ 0\\+\gamma\beta\  0\ 0\ \gamma}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="OwNkSi8z"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="AB=I"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">AB=I</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="ooo3t6w9">闵可夫斯基度规: <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="G=G'\quad"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">G=G'\quad</span></span></span><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="G=G^{-1}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">G=G^{-1}</span></span></span></p><p data-pid="0Bm6tYNL"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="1\\0\quad 1\\0\quad0\quad1\\ 0\quad 0\quad 0\quad -1"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">1\\0\quad 1\\0\quad0\quad1\\ 0\quad 0\quad 0\quad -1</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="zCFk-8s_"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="ds^2=dx_\mu dx^{\mu}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">ds^2=dx_\mu dx^{\mu}</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="x_\mu=(\textbf{x},-ct)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">x_\mu=(\textbf{x},-ct)</span></span></span> （协变）， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="x^\mu=(\textbf{x}, ct)^{T}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">x^\mu=(\textbf{x}, ct)^{T}</span></span></span> （逆变）</p><p data-pid="4dGnSRcl"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="|dx'&gt;=A|dx&gt;"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">|dx'&gt;=A|dx&gt;</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="&lt;dx|=&lt;dx'|A^{-1}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">&lt;dx|=&lt;dx'|A^{-1}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="kKGOSRN8">四维导数算符 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\partial_\mu=(\partial_x,\frac{1}{c}\partial_t)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\partial_\mu=(\partial_x,\frac{1}{c}\partial_t)</span></span></span> 协变，协变 逆变之间相互转化规则：空间项不变，时间项反号</p><p data-pid="PwvBFgje">矢量内积 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="A_\mu B^\mu=B_\nu A^\nu"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">A_\mu B^\mu=B_\nu A^\nu</span></span></span> （标量不变性）</p><p data-pid="f90uOQV7">物理规律与参考系无关：翻译: <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="A^\mu=B^\mu 或B_\mu A^\mu=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">A^\mu=B^\mu 或B_\mu A^\mu=0</span></span></span> ,A,B各代表协变or逆变矢量</p><p data-pid="99LflFP9">达朗贝尔算子 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\square^2=\partial_\mu\partial^\mu=\nabla^2-\frac{1}{c^2}\partial_t^2"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\square^2=\partial_\mu\partial^\mu=\nabla^2-\frac{1}{c^2}\partial_t^2</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="0gEAYvkx"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="d\phi=\partial_\mu\phi dx^\mu=dx_\mu\partial^\mu\phi"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">d\phi=\partial_\mu\phi dx^\mu=dx_\mu\partial^\mu\phi</span></span></span> </p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>8.四维旋度</h3><p data-pid="f3jmz01w">三维 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="B=\nabla \times A"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">B=\nabla \times A</span></span></span> <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="B_1=\partial_2A_3-\partial_3A_2"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">B_1=\partial_2A_3-\partial_3A_2</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="tRh1JLW6"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\phi=k\cdot x-\omega t"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\phi=k\cdot x-\omega t</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="k^\mu=(k,\omega/c)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">k^\mu=(k,\omega/c)</span></span></span> ，</p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><p data-pid="NNKUBzlr"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="k'^\mu=Ak^\nu"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">k'^\mu=Ak^\nu</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="kA4G1Abr">可以由此导出多普勒 光行差 <span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E8%8F%B2%E7%B4%A2&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">菲索<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>实验...</p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><p data-pid="I_n7SKvq"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="F_{\mu\nu}=\partial_\mu A_\nu-\partial_\nu A_\mu"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">F_{\mu\nu}=\partial_\mu A_\nu-\partial_\nu A_\mu</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="lkQmn7gm"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\partial_t\rho+\nabla\cdot j=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\partial_t\rho+\nabla\cdot j=0</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\partial_\mu=(\partial_{x},\partial_y,\partial_z,\frac{1}{c}\partial_t)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\partial_\mu=(\partial_{x},\partial_y,\partial_z,\frac{1}{c}\partial_t)</span></span></span> 把 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="j^\mu"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">j^\mu</span></span></span> 设成 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="(j^x,j^y,j^z,c\rho)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">(j^x,j^y,j^z,c\rho)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="2TSxSZNU"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\partial_\mu j^\mu=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\partial_\mu j^\mu=0</span></span></span> (协变性)</p><p data-pid="7N1fQmD-">达朗贝尔方程： <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\square^2A=-\mu_0j，\square^2\phi=-\frac{\rho}{\epsilon_0}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\square^2A=-\mu_0j，\square^2\phi=-\frac{\rho}{\epsilon_0}</span></span></span> ，合并令 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="A^\mu=(A_x,A_y,A_z,\phi/c)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">A^\mu=(A_x,A_y,A_z,\phi/c)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="oDo1p5D6">得到 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\square^2A^\mu=-\mu_0j^\mu"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\square^2A^\mu=-\mu_0j^\mu</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="ZtYiZLcw"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\partial_\mu A^\mu=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\partial_\mu A^\mu=0</span></span></span> (协变性), <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="F_{\mu\nu}=\partial_\mu A_\nu-\partial_\nu A_\mu"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">F_{\mu\nu}=\partial_\mu A_\nu-\partial_\nu A_\mu</span></span></span></p><p data-pid="W3G_-_TC">前三行列其实和三维没有区别，设计对t求偏导的磁矢势为0</p><figure data-size="normal"><div class="RichText-ConditionalImagePortal"><img src="https://pica.zhimg.com/v2-33169e6187e0f65a9fe1c4ba6332bb34_1440w.jpg" data-caption="" data-size="normal" data-rawwidth="1928" data-rawheight="1143" data-original="https://pica.zhimg.com/v2-33169e6187e0f65a9fe1c4ba6332bb34_r.jpg"></div></figure><p data-pid="m6ReX1zr"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="A'^\mu=a^\mu_\nu A^\nu"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">A'^\mu=a^\mu_\nu A^\nu</span></span></span>  to be continued</p><hr><p data-pid="cqoo6tYD">后记：王老师延续了一贯的出卷风格，题目只要认真做作业和看过书基本不会有陌生感。希望成绩还行</p><p data-pid="Fks8rUrq">强烈推荐同学们选王<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=258127630&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E5%B0%91%E6%9D%B0&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">少杰<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>老师的电动力学课程！（不知道为什么选的人这么少）</p><hr><p data-pid="JWWrj7Ms">最后记：期末卷面已经90+，总评96，王老师上课真的很好！列入大三申请助教重点考虑列表（</p>
